### [25] [The Linear Representation Hypothesis Needs a Group Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27158)
*Louie Hong Yao,Yuhao Li,Shengchao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用群作用统一线性表示假设（LRH）的等价性定义，将LRH视为一族假设而非单一假设，并用于审计可解释性分析中的表示量值，为LLM表示分析提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表示假设（LRH）缺乏对表示等价性的显式定义，导致不同度量、探针和干预可能对应不同的假设，无法泛化到不同训练模型。

Method: 论文为理论框架，无具体模型架构。其提出一个基于群作用的规范化框架：首先定义表示对象（如激活向量、嵌入），然后指定生成该表示的映射过程（如模型前向传播），最后声明所断言的性质（如线性可分）。同时考虑模型架构带来的等价性（如置换、旋转等群作用），从而精确界定不同度量、探针和干预所对应的假设。该框架用于审计现有表示量值和可解释性分析，检查它们是否隐含了不同的等价性假设。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出LRH是一族由表示等价性区分的假设，而非单一假设；用群作用形式化表示对象、生成过程和断言属性，考虑模型架构施加的等价性；建立审计框架，用于审计常见表示量值和近期可解释性分析中的假设一致性

Abstract: To make claims about representations that generalize beyond a particular trained model, we need to specify when two representations should count as equivalent. The Linear Representation Hypothesis is often discussed without making this equivalence explicit. Different notions of equivalence preserve different structures, so metrics, probes, and interventions that appear to study the same representation may in fact correspond to different hypotheses. We therefore argue that the Linear Representation Hypothesis is not one hypothesis but a family of claims distinguished by representation equivalence. We formalize this idea using group actions, specifying the representation object, the procedure that produces it, and the property ultimately asserted, while accounting for equivalences imposed by the model architecture. This framework clarifies how assumptions can change across metrics, reading points, and analysis stages, and we use it to audit common representation quantities and recent interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [26] [EBRL: Asynchronous Embodied RL by Multi-Grained Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27547)
*Liang Mi,Weijun Wang,Bowen Gao,Tianze Yu,Zixu Hao,Han Xiao,Xin Ding,Mingzhe Huang,Xin He,Lu Shi,Hao Wu,Haipeng Dai,Guihai Chen,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EBRL 是一个异步具身强化学习训练系统，通过异步流水线调度器和细粒度 CPU/GPU 资源管理器，消除 rollout 与训练间的同步停滞并动态匹配异构资源需求。在 RLinf 上使用四个具身策略和四个仿真基准、异构 GPU 测试床评估，相比 SOTA 具身 RL 系统实现 1.30-3.47 倍 rollout 吞吐和 2.5 倍训练收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身强化学习训练流程中环境模拟、动作生成与模型更新阶段对 CPU 和 GPU 的需求异构，传统同步式 rollout 与独占 GPU 分配导致大量硬件资源浪费与同步阻塞，难以高效利用异构资源。

Method: EBRL 是基于 RLinf 实现的异步具身强化学习训练系统。整体架构包含两个核心组件：（1）异步流水线调度器，负责将 rollout（环境模拟与动作生成）与训练重叠执行，跨不同环境组流水线化模拟和生成阶段，并让每个环境独立推进，从而消除同步屏障带来的停滞；（2）细粒度资源管理器，将 CPU 核心与 GPU 流多处理器（SM）作为可池化资源，利用各阶段 profiling 信息和运行时反馈，动态调整资源配额和批大小，以匹配环境模拟、动作生成和模型更新阶段对 CPU 与 GPU 的不同需求。数据流上，环境组独立产生模拟数据并驱动动作生成，生成的轨迹异步送入模型更新阶段，资源管理器根据各阶段实时负载重新分配 CPU/GPU 资源。论文未披露训练目标函数、具体模型结构、参数规模、硬件型号、耗时等细节。推理/服务路径未单独说明，系统在异构 GPU 测试床上与基线系统进行了吞吐与收敛速度的对比实验。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "端到端 rollout 吞吐", "value": "1.30-3.47 倍", "setting": "在 RLinf 上实现，使用四个具身策略与四个仿真基准，在异构 GPU 测试床上与 SOTA 具身 RL 系统对比"}, {"metric": "训练收敛速度", "value": "2.5 倍", "setting": "在 RLinf 上实现，使用四个具身策略与四个仿真基准，在异构 GPU 测试床上与 SOTA 具身 RL 系统对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 异步流水线调度器：将 rollout 与训练重叠，跨环境组流水线化模拟与生成，每个环境独立执行，消除同步停滞。；细粒度资源管理器：池化 CPU 核心与 GPU 流多处理器（SM），利用阶段画像和运行时反馈动态调整资源配额与批大小，以适应各阶段变化的需求。

Abstract: Embodied reinforcement learning (RL) improves model capabilities with a pipeline of environment simulation, action generation, and model updates. These stages show heterogeneous CPU and GPU demands, making efficient resource utilization difficult. Recent systems overlap rollout (simulation and generation) with training for efficiency, but exclusive GPU allocation and synchronized barrier in rollout still leave substantial hardware resource waste. In this paper, we present EBRL, an asynchronous embodied RL training system with two core techniques. The asynchronous pipelined scheduler overlaps rollout and training, pipelines simulation and generation across environment groups, and carries out each environment independently, eliminating synchronization stalls. The fine-grained resource manager pools CPU cores and GPU streaming multiprocessors, and uses stage profiles and runtime feedback to adjust resource quotas and batch sizes to meet the shifting demands among stages. We implement EBRL on RLinf and evaluate it with four embodied policies and four simulation benchmarks across heterogeneous GPU testbeds. Experiments show that EBRL achieves 1.30-3.47 times the end-to-end rollout throughput and 2.5 times of training convergency compared to the SOTA embodied RL systems.

</details>


### [27] [Counterfactual Constraint-Conditioned On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27421)
*Yanzhao Zheng,Yuanqiang Yu,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Zhentao Zhang,Jihuai Zhu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CC-OPD通过反事实约束条件化蒸馏，利用教师模型在约束被移除时的概率变化构造token级塑形奖励，无需外部验证器，在多约束指令遵循任务上平均性能最佳，1.5B学生模型超越7B RL教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多约束指令遵循中，强指令微调模型仍会违反部分约束，现有方法（RL或单教师蒸馏）在约束增多时概率质量稀释，导致约束违反。

Method: CC-OPD在标准on-policy蒸馏（OPD）基础上扩展。学生模型生成响应后，教师模型在完整约束条件下提供token级概率分布作为基线奖励（vanilla OPD）。同时，CC-OPD对教师进行多次前向传播，每次从条件中移除一个约束，得到每个约束被移除时的逐token概率。通过比较完整条件与移除某约束后的概率，计算逐token对数似然偏移（即该约束对每个token的贡献）。这些偏移被求和、裁剪，并加到vanilla OPD奖励上作为token级塑形项。整个过程仅使用冻结的教师模型，无需外部验证器。训练目标为最大化塑形后的奖励，本质是策略梯度或强化学习目标。推理时学生模型独立生成，不使用教师。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均得分", "value": "最高", "setting": "两个Qwen模型对、七个基准测试，CC-OPD在所有评估的学生训练方法中平均得分最高"}, {"metric": "MulDimIF基准表现", "value": "1.5B学生模型超越其7B RL训练教师", "setting": "两个Qwen模型对、七个基准测试之一（MulDimIF）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 反转监督生成方向：从教师条件中逐个消除约束，构造反事实约束条件化信号；利用冻结教师模型计算逐token留一法对数似然偏移，作为token级塑形项；将塑形项裁剪后加到vanilla OPD奖励中，无需外部验证器，且在聚合偏移为零时退化为vanilla OPD

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires a model to respond to a query under many simultaneously active constraints. Even strong instruction-tuned models still routinely violate some of them. Existing approaches either augment supervision with sequence- or token-level RL rewards from external verifiers or learned graders, or use on-policy distillation (OPD) against a single full-context teacher whose probability mass becomes diluted as more constraints become simultaneously active. We propose CC-OPD (Counterfactual Constraint-Conditioned On-Policy Distillation), which inverts the standard supervision-generation direction in distillation. Rather than enriching the teacher with information beyond what the student sees, CC-OPD ablates each constraint from the teacher's conditioning in turn, and constructs the per-constraint signal from the resulting per-token probability differentials. The resulting per-token leave-one-out log-likelihood shifts are summed, clipped, and added to the vanilla OPD reward as a token-level shaping term. All shaping terms are obtained from the frozen teacher, without an external verifier during distillation, and the reward equals vanilla OPD wherever the aggregate shift is zero. Across two Qwen model pairs and seven benchmarks, CC-OPD achieves the highest average among all evaluated student-training methods. A 1.5B student trained with CC-OPD surpasses its own 7B RL-trained teacher on the MulDimIF benchmark.

</details>


### [28] [Memory Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28399)
*Jiale Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory Attention 用逐层 token-indexed memory 表替代注意力的专用 value projection，value 由 memory 查表结果与上下文 key 组合而成；推理时归一化可折叠进 memory 表，使 value 构造变为查表加加法，并支持 CPU offloading 与预取以降低 GPU 显存占用。在匹配训练 token 预算下，语言建模与下游平均表现均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力中 value 由上下文隐藏态动态投影产生，即使部分内容可跨上下文复用也需重复计算与存储；论文探索能否用 token-indexed memory 替代专用 value projection，同时保持上下文依赖性并降低推理成本。

Method: Memory Attention 的输入为 token 序列。与传统注意力不同，value 不再仅由上下文隐藏态经专用 value projection 生成，而是由 layer-specific token memory 表按 token index 检索得到 token 特定表示，再与 contextual keys 结合，从而在复用 token 级内容的同时保留上下文依赖。每一层拥有独立的 token memory 表，memory 提供静态可复用部分，key 提供动态上下文部分，二者组合形成最终 value 参与注意力计算。训练目标仍为语言建模，在匹配的训练 token 预算下进行，并引入额外的 memory 参数。推理阶段，由于 value 构造中的归一化项可预先折叠进 memory 表，前向计算中的 value 构造可简化为对 memory 表的 lookup 与加法操作。进一步，token-indexed retrieval 使 memory 表可按 token 索引从 CPU 内存获取，因此可将部分参数放在 CPU 上并通过 prefetching 预取，减少 GPU 上需常驻的参数存储，缓解显存压力。论文未披露具体模型规模、层数、memory 表大小、训练硬件与推理延迟等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "language modeling performance", "value": "improved", "setting": "matched training token budgets，额外 memory 参数，多种 attention 配置"}, {"metric": "average downstream performance", "value": "improved", "setting": "matched training token budgets，额外 memory 参数，多种 attention 配置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Memory Attention（MA）：value 由 layer-specific token memory 与 contextual keys 组合构造，memory 提供 token 特定表示，keys 保留上下文依赖；推理期将归一化折叠进 memory 表，使 value 构造退化为查表与加法；token-indexed retrieval 支持 CPU offloading 与 prefetching，减少 GPU 参数存储

Abstract: Language models typically construct attention values from contextual hidden states, even when some of their content may be reusable across contexts. We investigate whether token-indexed memory can replace the dedicated value projection when complemented by contextual information. We propose Memory Attention (MA), which forms values by combining layer-specific token memory with contextual keys. The memory supplies token-specific representations, while the keys preserve context dependence. At inference, normalization can be folded into the memory tables, reducing value construction to lookup and addition. Token-indexed retrieval also enables CPU offloading with prefetching, reducing GPU parameter storage. Under matched training token budgets and with additional memory parameters, experiments across attention configurations show improved language modeling and average downstream performance.

</details>


### [29] [Resource-Adaptive Stochastic Gradient Descent for Online Linear Programming without Re-solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28263)
*Jiameng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RASGD，一种资源自适应的随机梯度下降算法，用于在线线性规划，无需重复求解LP。每次到达仅需O(m)操作和内存，理论保证O(log T)遗憾界，实验表明其分配质量接近逐到达LP重求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线线性规划问题中，传统重求解（re-solving）方法计算成本高，需要一种无需重复求解LP、计算高效且具有理论保证的算法。

Method: RASGD是一种用于随机在线线性规划的一阶随机梯度下降算法。输入：每个时间步到达一个请求（如广告展示机会）及当前资源库存状态。主要模块：(1) 资源定价模块：维护资源价格向量（对偶变量），每次到达时根据当前剩余库存计算资源允许量，并更新对偶目标中的剩余库存余量；(2) SGD更新模块：使用单个请求和当前库存信息，按照一阶梯度更新资源价格，步长采用先递减后递增的调度，以在早期充分学习、后期快速匹配库存调整；(3) 分配决策模块：基于当前资源价格和请求特征，决定是否分配资源及分配量。数据流：每个请求到达→读取当前库存和价格→计算更新方向→更新价格→做出分配决策→更新库存。训练目标：最小化相对于事后分数最优解的期望遗憾。推理/服务流：在线逐请求处理，无需批量重求解LP。该架构通过将重求解逻辑嵌入一阶更新，解决了计算效率与分配质量之间的权衡问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将重求解的当前资源定价逻辑表达为一阶SGD更新，每次到达仅用请求和当前库存更新资源价格；步长先减后增，匹配早期学习与后期库存调整速度的调度策略；在非退化条件下证明每个样本路径可行，并达到O(log T)期望遗憾界，匹配已知分布策略的下界；无需跟踪变化资源水平下的最优价格，通过曲率分析将参考价格附近的曲率转化为库存稳定性

Abstract: The growth of large language model (LLM) inference and search services increases the scale of online linear programming problems, motivating computationally efficient algorithms. We develop resource-adaptive stochastic gradient descent (RASGD) for stochastic online linear programming. The algorithm uses one request and current inventory to update resource prices, requiring O(m) operations for m resources and memory per arrival and no LP or sample-average optimization. The central idea is to express the current-resource pricing logic of re-solving through a first-order SGD update: each arrival refreshes the remaining-inventory allowance in the dual objective, while the stepsize decreases for early learning and increases later to match the speed of inventory adjustment. Under standard non-degeneracy conditions, our algorithm is feasible on every sample path and achieves O(\log T) expected regret against the realized fractional hindsight optimum, which matches the lower bound, even for policies that know the distribution and have unrestricted computation. The analysis converts curvature around the fixed reference price into inventory stability without tracking optimal prices at changing resource levels. Numerical experiments show that RASGD achieves regret competitive with per-arrival LP re-solving and improves upon the tested first-order baselines, while retaining the computational efficiency of first-order methods. These results establish RASGD as a computationally efficient approach to achieving high allocation quality in large-scale OLP.

</details>


### [30] [hyperbolix: Hyperbolic Deep Learning in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28248)
*Timo Klein,Thomas Lang,Yllka Velaj,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: hyperbolix 是首个通用 JAX 双曲深度学习开源库，基于 Flax NNX，支持六种流形、覆盖线性/卷积/注意力/归一化/向量量化等层族，并包含 Riemannian 优化器、分布与降维工具；其无消去公式解决了双曲面两点操作在 float32 下的精度崩溃问题。对需要层次化表示与可扩展嵌入的推荐/检索系统具有基础构件价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双曲深度学习实现分散、缺乏通用接口，且标准双曲公式在远离原点时因大数相减导致 float32 精度崩溃，难以在统一框架下构建和训练双曲神经网络。

Method: hyperbolix 以 JAX 与 Flax NNX 为基础，整体架构分为四层。第一层是流形层：实现欧氏空间、Poincaré 球、双曲面、κ-立体投影、混合曲率乘积空间和固有速度空间共六种流形，统一接口；流形设计为无状态，曲率在调用时传入，流形操作作用于单点，批量操作由 JAX 自动向量化（vmap）实现，从而保持函数式纯度并便于组合。第二层是几何操作与数值稳定层：提供指数映射、对数映射、平行移动、距离等基本操作，并针对双曲面上两点距离等操作因大数相减导致精度退化的缺陷，采用无消去（cancellation-free）重构公式，使其在远离原点区域于 float32 下保持稳定，避免 NaN。第三层是层族：基于流形操作构建线性层、卷积、注意力、归一化、位置编码、回归和向量量化等模块，覆盖 Ganea 原始双曲神经网络、Hypformer、Lorentzian ResNet 等完全双曲架构；层族与流形解耦，可互换替换。第四层是训练与概率层：Riemannian 优化器以 optax 变换形式实现，可与 JAX 训练循环无缝集成；并提供包裹分布（wrapped distributions）与双曲降维技术。在数据流上，输入先被映射到所选流形（通过指数映射等），经流形上的层族进行特征变换、注意力与归一化，最终在流形上或切空间中得到表示；损失与优化器在流形几何下执行。测试层面，每个受检操作均与源论文闭式 NumPy/SciPy 转写或有限差分对齐，覆盖 float32 与 float64，以保证数值正确性。该架构通过无状态流形、统一接口与批量 vmap，解决了双曲模型实现碎片化与数值不稳定两大问题，使推荐/检索等场景下的层次化表示学习能够以可复用构件形式落地。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首个通用 JAX 双曲深度学习库 hyperbolix，基于 Flax NNX 与 optax，统一支持六种流形（欧氏空间、Poincaré 球、双曲面、κ-立体投影、混合曲率乘积空间、固有速度空间）的通用接口。；提供覆盖线性层、卷积、注意力、归一化、位置编码、回归和向量量化的层族，涵盖 Ganea 原始双曲神经网络到 Hypformer、Lorentzian ResNet 等近期完全双曲架构。；针对双曲面上的两点操作（如距离）提出无消去公式，避免大数相减，在远离原点处实现 float32 下仍精确，而先前实现会返回 NaN。；每个受检操作均对照源论文的闭式 NumPy/SciPy 转写或有限差分进行 float32 与 float64 精度测试。

Abstract: We present hyperbolix, an open-source library for hyperbolic deep learning in JAX, built on Flax NNX. To our knowledge, it is the first comprehensive, general-purpose hyperbolic deep learning library in JAX. It includes six manifolds with a common interface: Euclidean space, the Poincaré ball, the hyperboloid, the $\kappa$-stereographic model, mixed-curvature product spaces, and the proper velocity space. We implement layer families that cover linear layers, convolutions, attention, normalization, positional encoding, regression, and vector quantization. These building blocks span methods ranging from Ganea's original hyperbolic neural networks to recent fully hyperbolic architectures such as Hypformer and Lorentzian ResNet. Additionally, hyperbolix contains Riemannian optimizers implemented as optax transformations, wrapped distributions, and hyperbolic dimensionality-reduction techniques. Its API uses idiomatic JAX: Manifolds are stateless, with curvature being passed at call time, while manifold operations act on single points, with this http URL enabling batch operations. The precision of every checked operation is tested against a closed-form NumPy/SciPy transcription from the source paper or a finite difference, for both float32 and float64. On the hyperboloid, standard formulas for two-point operations, such as the distance, lose precision far from the origin, because they subtract two large, nearly equal terms. hyperbolix replaces these subtractions with cancellation-free formulas that stay accurate in float32 at distances where prior implementations return NaN. hyperbolix is available under the MIT license at this https URL .

</details>


### [31] [Support-Compiled Feature Folding: More Evidence at Lower Memory Across Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28208)
*Tian Zhou,Beverly Jin,Xue Wang,Linxiao Yang,Wenwei Wang,Bingqing Peng,Mengni Ye,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向表格基础模型的训练无关推理框架SCFF，通过支持度编译的特征折叠将二次特征交互转为线性宽度工作，在固定显存预算下保留更多证据，在18个数据集上于六个骨干模型上一致提升精度和NLL，显存节省最高达2.36倍，峰值比达34.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在特征侧扩展时面临困境：全宽两两混合的计算量随列数二次增长，而特征选择虽节省内存却丢弃证据，导致精度损失。

Method: SCFF是一个训练无关的推理框架，作用于冻结的表格基础模型骨干。输入为表格特征，首先对特征按支持度排序。排序后的特征被路由通过原生特征编码器的有界叶节点（即受限的局部工作集），这避免了全宽两两混合的二次增长。在编码路径中，SCFF对残差证据进行支持度检查，确保不丢失重要信息。编码后的消息在进入单一上下文预测器之前被合并，从而将特征交互工作从二次复杂度降低为线性于宽度，并且局部工作集有界。整个推理过程不进行预测集成，也不训练新参数，最终输出基于合并消息的单个上下文预测。该方法通过支持度编译和有界路由，在低内存下保留更多证据，提升精度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "dataset-macro accuracy 和 NLL", "value": "在所有六个评估的骨干模型上均提升", "setting": "18个数据集宽表切片，固定AMLB-29、TabZilla和TabArena快照"}, {"metric": "相对误差降低", "value": "最高达26.1%", "setting": "四个匹配宽度比较，锁定折叠上的95%数据集自助区间"}, {"metric": "中位配对GPU显存节省", "value": "2.09倍至2.36倍", "setting": "配对比较"}, {"metric": "分别观察到的最大峰值比率", "value": "34.3倍", "setting": "显存峰值比较"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "4.06和3.72个百分点", "setting": "在测量的显存峰值上限下，对比最宽可行单叶，预声明的TabICLv2和TabPFN-3宽核心分层"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Support-Compiled Feature Folding (SCFF)：训练无关的推理框架，不改变冻结骨干网络。；将支持度排序的特征通过原生特征编码器的有界叶节点进行路由，并支持度检查残差证据。；在单次上下文预测前合并编码消息，把二次特征交互工作转换为线性宽度工作，且工作集有界。；无需集成预测或训练新参数，在固定显存预算下通过保留更多支持度选择的证据提升精度。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models face a feature-side scaling dilemma: full-width pairwise mixing grows quadratically with the number of columns, whereas feature selection saves memory by discarding evidence. We introduce Support-Compiled Feature Folding (SCFF), a training-free inference framework that resolves this dilemma without changing the frozen backbone. SCFF routes support-ranked features through bounded leaves of the native feature encoder, support-checks the residual evidence, and merges the encoded messages before a single contextual prediction. It thereby converts quadratic feature-interaction work into linear-in-width work with a bounded local working set, without ensembling predictions or training new parameters. On the exhaustive 18-dataset wide-table slice of fixed AMLB-29, TabZilla, and TabArena snapshots, SCFF improves dataset-macro accuracy and NLL on all six evaluated backbones. All four matched-width comparisons retain favorable 95 percent dataset-bootstrap intervals on locked folds, with relative error reductions up to 26.1 percent. Median paired GPU-memory savings are 2.09x to 2.36x, and the ratio of separately observed maximum peaks reaches 34.3x. Under a measured peak-memory ceiling, SCFF uses the saved budget to preserve more support-selected evidence, improving accuracy by 4.06 and 3.72 points over the widest feasible single leaf on predeclared wide-Core strata of TabICLv2 and TabPFN-3.

</details>


### [32] [RL Starts before RL: On Policy Distillation for Better Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28145)
*Shuai Dong,Yongfu Zhu,Yuqi Xu,Weichu Xie,Liuwenpu,Ziyue Wang,Kaiwen Tuo,Congcong Wang,Siyuan Wang,Wenqi Shao,Shuai Yang,Ji Zhao,Caoyuan Ma,Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Xinlei Yu,Hongrui Wu,Xiaoxuan He,Fangke Chen,Dianyi Wang,Kanghui Tian,Sirry Chen,Xingyu Liu,Xiangnan Wu,Jiawei Guo,Haowen Hou,LingHan Chen,Zhongyu Wei,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究将在线策略蒸馏(OPD)作为强化学习的前置准备阶段，发现OPD初始化的学生模型在后续RL中能达到更高最终性能，且该优势不完全由初始准确率解释。论文进一步分析蒸馏目标和轨迹来源对后续RL的影响，揭示reverse-KL与forward-KL的优劣随训练阶段变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的最终性能依赖于初始策略，如何通过在线策略蒸馏(OPD)作为RL前的准备阶段来提升后续RL效果，以及蒸馏目标、轨迹来源如何影响该增益。

Method: 论文提出将在线策略蒸馏(OPD)作为RL训练的准备阶段。输入为学生模型在蒸馏轨迹上的输出分布与教师模型分布。主模块包括：1) 策略蒸馏阶段，学生模型在教师生成的或学生自身生成的轨迹上，通过KL散度目标对齐教师分布；2) RL阶段，以蒸馏后的学生作为初始策略，使用结果反馈优化。数据流为：轨迹来源(教师生成或学生生成)经蒸馏目标(KL方向选择reverse-KL或forward-KL)训练学生，再将学生送入下游RL。训练目标为在蒸馏阶段最小化选定方向的KL散度，RL阶段最大化结果奖励。推理/服务流程未明确说明。核心问题是选择恰当的蒸馏目标与轨迹来源以最大化RL后性能，实验表明该选择依赖于后续训练阶段。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "RL后最终性能", "value": "高于直接RL或SFT+RL", "setting": "共享RL设置下，学生模型由OPD初始化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明OPD作为RL准备阶段可提升最终RL性能，且该优势可独立于初始准确率提升而存在；发现Pre-RL Pass@k无法完全解释OTD增益，行为分析指向与教师分布超越top-1一致性的对齐；揭示蒸馏目标与轨迹来源的交互：标准reverse-KL在RL前更好但被forward-KL在RL后反超，教师生成轨迹时reverse-KL始终领先

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) improves reasoning, but its performance depends on the policy from which training begins. We study on-policy distillation (OPD) as a preparation stage for RL and ask whether its benefits extend beyond improvements in the distilled model's initial accuracy. Under shared RL settings, students initialized with OPD reach higher final performance than those trained with direct RL or supervised fine-tuning followed by RL. This advantage can emerge even when OPD produces little immediate improvement in accuracy. Pre-RL Pass@k does not fully explain the benefit: similar or even higher values do not necessarily lead to better performance after RL. Behavioral analyses point to alignment with the teacher's distribution beyond top-1 agreement as a possible explanation. Such alignment may favor higher-quality reasoning paths while retaining alternatives that RL can further refine using outcome feedback. We further examine how trajectory sources and divergence objectives affect the value of distillation for subsequent RL. Standard reverse-KL OPD performs better before RL, but forward-KL OPD overtakes it afterward; with teacher-generated distillation trajectories, reverse KL remains ahead at both stages. These findings suggest that the preferred distillation objective depends on both the trajectory source and the training that follows. Our results support evaluating OPD as preparation for RL and selecting distillation choices by the performance achieved after subsequent training.

</details>


### [33] [Shared Global KV with Layer-Specific Local History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28006)
*Xinglang Xian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究共享全局 KV cache 下应保留何种局部记忆，提出保留历史内容的层特异性局部分支，在 126M/2K 的 8 seed 实验中比当前 token 局部分支降低约 1.4% perplexity；与 GQA、相邻层共享相比同源似然更优，但 cache 更大、长请求延迟更高，并给出可减少上层构建工作的后缀调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在层间共享全局 KV cache 以减少存储时，如何通过局部记忆（特别是历史内容 vs 当前输入来源）恢复跨层表示多样性并保持建模质量。

Method: 面向 decoder-only Transformer 的生成缓存。共享全局 KV 旨在跨层复用键值以减少存储，但会降低跨深度表示多样性。本文在共享全局 KV 之外引入层特异性局部历史：将历史内容与用于形成它的输入来源分离，局部分支保留历史内容而非仅当前 token。在 126M、2K 上下文设置下进行 8 seed 研究，发现保留历史内容的局部历史比当前 token 局部分支取得约 1.4% 更低的 held-out test perplexity；capacity、entry-count 和训练计算控制实验支持历史内容本身的价值。进一步在相邻层共享局部输入但保留独立投影的两 seed 比较中，历史收益仍存，且共享来源可缩短精确 cache 构建依赖。与 GQA 及相邻层 KV 共享相比，在等预算学习率搜索和新种子确认下，同源似然更优，但 cache 更大、长请求延迟更高。等 token 适配到 8K 后，相对相邻层共享的顺序保持，短上下文有代价。作者推导了一个充分后缀调度，可减少上层构建工作量并在精确算术下保持完整 cache。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "held-out test perplexity", "value": "降低约 1.4%（相对当前 token 局部分支）", "setting": "126M 参数，2K 上下文，8 seed"}, {"metric": "same-source likelihood", "value": "优于 GQA 和相邻层 KV 共享（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "等 bounded learning-rate 搜索，新种子确认"}, {"metric": "长请求延迟", "value": "高于 GQA/相邻层共享（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "相同同源似然比较设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 分离局部记忆中的历史内容与形成它的输入来源，明确局部分支应保留历史而非仅当前 token。；提出带层特异性局部历史的共享全局 KV 架构，并用 8 seed 研究验证约 1.4% 的 held-out test perplexity 下降。；设计 capacity、entry-count、training-compute 控制实验，支持历史内容本身的价值。；在相邻层共享局部输入来源但保留独立投影时，历史收益仍存在，并缩短精确 cache 构建依赖。；与 GQA 及相邻层 KV 共享在等 bounded LR 搜參下对比，以更大 cache、更高长请求延迟换取同源似然提升。；等 token 适配 8K 后，相对相邻层共享的顺序保持，但短上下文有代价。；推导出充分后缀调度，减少上层构建工作量并在精确算术下保持完整 cache。

Abstract: Decoder-only Transformer language models cache keys and values (KV) to reuse past computation during generation. Sharing KV across layers saves storage but reduces the diversity of representations available across depth. We study what local memory should retain alongside shared global KV, separating historical content from the input source used to form it. At 126M parameters and 2K context, an eight-seed study finds about 1.4% lower held-out test perplexity with local history than with a current-token local branch. Capacity, entry-count and training-compute controls support the value of historical content. In a two-seed comparison, this value persists when adjacent layers share local inputs while retaining independent projections; source sharing also shortens exact cache-construction dependencies. Against GQA and adjacent-layer KV sharing, equal bounded learning-rate searches and new-seed confirmation yield better same-source likelihood with larger caches and higher long-request latency. The ordering against adjacent-layer sharing persists after equal-token adaptation to 8K, with a short-context cost. The eight-seed external-book history effect remains uncertain, and downstream outcomes vary by task. We derive a sufficient suffix schedule that reduces upper-layer construction work while preserving the complete cache in exact arithmetic.

</details>


### [34] [Learning from Failures: Heterogeneous Graph Memory for Small Language Model Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28003)
*Jiaxing Li,Lei Song,Rui Dong,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRESH 将历史成功与失败构建为异构图记忆，帮助冻结的小语言模型在 τ-Bench 和 AppWorld 上提升工具使用可靠性与任务成功率，为智能体外部记忆与可靠性提供思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在长时程、有状态的工具使用环境中易产生结构性错误（遗漏必要观察、过早写入、重复失败调用、违反动作前置条件），现有微调方法需要大量数据和算力，而平坦记忆无法保留失败动作的因果上下文与安全条件。

Method: FRESH 将历史交互中的任务、动作、错误、修复和执行条件建模为异构图，利用图中显式建模的依赖关系保存失败动作的因果上下文与安全条件。检索时，冻结的小语言模型根据当前状态从异构图中获取结构化的成功与失败经验，从而复用可靠策略、规避重复失败并作出更安全的决策。论文未披露具体架构细节、训练目标或推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: FRESH：基于经验结构化异构图（failure-aware retrieval over experience-structured heterogeneous graphs）的失败感知检索框架。；显式建模任务、动作、错误、修复与执行条件之间的依赖关系，将历史成功与失败转化为结构化外部经验。；面向冻结语言模型提供可复用可靠策略、避免重复失败并支持更安全决策的检索机制。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized language models offer cost-effective executors for tool-using agents, making them attractive for local and large-scale deployment. However, in long-horizon and stateful environments, they often make structural errors such as missing required observations, performing premature writes, repeating failed calls, and violating action preconditions. These errors can lead to incorrect state updates, policy violations, and costly or irreversible consequences, making reliable tool execution a critical deployment challenge. Existing fine-tuning approaches require substantial data and computation, while flat memory may retrieve failed actions without preserving their causal context or safety conditions. In this paper, we propose FRESH, a Failure-aware Retrieval framework over Experience-Structured Heterogeneous graphs, which transforms historical successes and failures into structured external experience for tool-using agents. By explicitly modeling the dependencies among tasks, actions, errors, repairs, and execution conditions, FRESH helps frozen language models reuse reliable strategies, avoid recurring failures, and make safer decisions in stateful tool interactions. Experiments on $\tau$-Bench and AppWorld with multiple open-source models show that FRESH consistently improves task success and tool-use reliability over no-memory agents and representative memory-based baselines.

</details>

*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Fariya Afrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出置信集加权保形预测（CS-WCP），在 LLM judge 部署流量中组比例不确定时，通过构造组质量同时置信区间并返回兼容比例下加权保形集的并集，实现保守的尾部覆盖保护。在 336 个构造型共享支持偏移实验中平均覆盖 0.973，优于源保形预测的 0.954，但集合略大；自然跨任务迁移下覆盖从 0.882 升至 0.962。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当部署流量改变任务或策略组的比例时，基于 LLM judge 的预测集合可能发生覆盖不足（undercoverage）。

Method: CS-WCP 以 LLM judge 输出的分数或标签预测为基础。针对部署流量中任务或策略组比例可能变化的情形，假设存在一个固定或独立学到的有限分组划分 S。对于每个可能的组比例向量，方法利用加权保形预测构造预测集；由于组比例未知，仅为源域与目标域组质量构建同时精确区间（置信集），然后对置信集内所有兼容比例向量返回加权保形集的并集。该并集在 O(G|Y|) 时间内通过线性端点规则计算。理论分析给出覆盖概率至少为 1-alpha-delta_w-tau_A-kappa，其中 alpha 为误覆盖率，delta_w 为权重估计误差，tau_A 为单元内协变量不匹配，kappa 为条件偏移。推理时无需重新训练 LLM judge，仅对已有 judge 输出进行保形集后处理。

Conclusion: 提出置信集加权保形预测（CS-WCP），在组比例不确定时构造源域与目标域组质量的同时精确区间。；返回所有兼容比例向量下加权保形集的并集，作为对不确定混合权重的鲁棒覆盖保护。；设计线性端点规则，在 O(G|Y|) 时间内计算该鲁棒并集。；理论上给出覆盖下界 1-alpha-delta_w-tau_A-kappa，并以 tau_A 度量单元内协变量不匹配、kappa 度量条件偏移。

Abstract: Prediction sets built from an LLM judge can undercover when deployment traffic changes the prevalence of task or policy groups. Weighted conformal prediction is exact under covariate shift when the density ratio is known, but group proportions must usually be estimated from finite unlabeled samples. We introduce confidence-set weighted conformal prediction (CS-WCP), which constructs simultaneous exact intervals for source and target group masses and returns the union of weighted conformal sets over every compatible ratio vector. For a fixed or independently learned finite partition, CS-WCP attains coverage at least 1-alpha-delta_w-tau_A-kappa, where tau_A measures within-cell covariate mismatch and kappa measures conditional shift. A linear endpoint rule computes the robust union in O(G|Y|) time. Across 336 constructed shared-support traffic shifts, CS-WCP reaches 0.973 mean coverage with 13 point failures, compared with 0.954 and 44 failures for source conformal prediction, at mean binary set sizes 1.74 and 1.65. On 336 natural cross-task transfers, coverage rises from 0.882 to 0.962, but mean set size reaches 1.87 and a size-matched group plug-in baseline is competitive. The method therefore supplies an auditable coverage safeguard under uncertain mixture weights; its value is conservative tail protection, not scalar probability calibration or uniformly smaller sets.

</details>

<details>

Conclusion: 将序列编码中的平衡树递归算子扩展到自回归预测，使所有前缀表示可在对数深度内计算。；实现线性运行时下的并行执行，同时保留循环模型线性深度的优势。；初步实验证明该模型类具备稳健的长度外推能力，性能接近基于ALiBi的Transformer。

Abstract: Language modeling using Transformers has become commonplace despite their fixed computational depth and quadratic runtime with respect to input tokens. Recurrent models on the other hand offer linear depth but no parallel execution. In this work, we extend balanced-tree recursive operators from sequence encoding to autoregressive prediction, enabling all prefix representations to be computed with logarithmic depth and linear runtime. Our experiments provide an initial characterization of this model class, demonstrating robust length extrapolation and performance approaching that of ALiBi-based Transformers, highlighting its potential as an alternative architecture for language modeling.

</details>

*Pit Neitemeier,Jiaze Li,Alessio Serra,Philipp Scholl,Sohir Maskey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出EQB和LEI两种方法，分别解决MoE训练中的全局和局部负载均衡问题。EQB以极小通信开销计算精确全局BF16分位数，LEI将局部负载误差注入路由器梯度。在7.5B参数MoE上训练500B tokens，EQB提升全局平衡和下游性能，LEI提升局部平衡并优于GShard损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 训练中需要同时实现全局负载均衡（防止专家利用率不足）和局部负载均衡（高效专家并行执行）。现有分布式分位数平衡（QB）使用依赖分片或近似的全局分位数，而token无关的专家偏置无法保证微批次级别的平衡。

Method: 论文针对MoE训练中的负载均衡问题，提出EQB和LEI两种方法。EQB在分布式训练中计算精确的全局批次BF16分位数，以极小的通信开销替代分片依赖或近似的全局分位数，从而改善全局负载均衡。LEI将局部负载误差直接注入到路由器分数的梯度中，以增强微批次级别的局部负载均衡。在7.5B参数MoE模型上使用最高500B tokens进行训练，EQB在全局平衡和下游性能上优于朴素QB，LEI在局部平衡上优于GShard损失且在可比质量下表现更好。端到端流程包括：输入token经过路由器计算专家分配分数，通过EQB进行全局分位数均衡，通过LEI将局部负载误差注入梯度以优化路由器参数，最终实现全局与局部负载均衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "全局平衡", "value": "优于朴素QB", "setting": "7.5B参数MoE，训练最高500B tokens"}, {"metric": "下游性能", "value": "优于朴素QB", "setting": "7.5B参数MoE，训练最高500B tokens"}, {"metric": "局部平衡", "value": "改善", "setting": "7.5B参数MoE，训练最高500B tokens"}, {"metric": "下游性能", "value": "在可比质量下优于GShard loss", "setting": "7.5B参数MoE，训练最高500B tokens"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Exact Quantile Balancing (EQB)：以极小的通信开销计算精确的全局批次BF16分位数。；Load-Error Injection (LEI)：将局部负载误差直接注入到路由器分数的梯度中。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training requires global load balance to prevent expert under-utilization and local balance for efficient expert-parallel execution. Existing distributed Quantile Balancing (QB) uses shard-dependent or approximate global quantiles, while token-independent expert biases cannot ensure microbatch-level balance. We introduce Exact Quantile Balancing (EQB), which computes exact global-batch BF16 quantiles with negligible communication, and Load-Error Injection (LEI), which injects local load errors directly into router-score gradients. On 7.5B-parameter MoEs trained for up to 500B tokens, EQB improves global balance and downstream performance over naive QB, while LEI improves local balance and outperforms the GShard loss at comparable quality.

</details>

*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Senjian Zhang,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VCRT通过形式化验证器构建交换性证书和反菱形监督，改进了可验证奖励强化学习中序列化顺序被误判为逻辑依赖的问题，在ProofWriter、CLRS和Lean的留一环境转移评估中取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可验证奖励强化学习中，训练数据中的序列化选择可能被错误地视为逻辑依赖，导致策略学习到错误的顺序约束，而非真正的交换性规则。

Method: VCRT方法首先通过重放相邻操作对来构建交换性证书：如果两个顺序都被验证器接受并达到相同的规范状态，则形成交换性证书；如果顺序被拒绝或状态改变，则形成反菱形。这些反菱形用于保留真正的先决条件，避免将序列化误认为依赖。策略优化时，将每个认证轨道的总概率质量作为信用分配的基础，而不是单独的轨迹。此外，方法施加了交换后一致性、源保留和策略漂移的约束。训练在共享的匿名关系图接口上进行，评估采用留一环境转移，所有训练和检查点决策在保留评估前冻结，评估使用每个项目一条贪心轨迹，无搜索或验证器反馈。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro pass rate", "value": "77.60%", "setting": "跨ProofWriter、CLRS、Lean的留一环境转移评估，单条贪心轨迹"}, {"metric": "paired gain", "value": "13.06 points", "setting": "与最强匹配基线相比，95% bootstrap CI [12.58, 13.54]"}, {"metric": "Lean gain", "value": "33.49 points", "setting": "Lean环境上的提升"}, {"metric": "ProofWriter和CLRS平均提升", "value": "2.85 points", "setting": "ProofWriter和CLRS上的平均提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Verifier-Certified Rule Transport (VCRT)，通过重放相邻操作对并利用原生验证器构建交换性证书和反菱形。；使用反菱形监督来保留真正的先决条件，并将策略信用分配给每个认证轨道的总概率质量。；在交换后施加一致性、源保留和策略漂移约束。；采用留一环境转移评估，使用共享的匿名关系图接口。

Abstract: Many computations admit several valid execution orders because independent subgoals or disjoint state updates can commute. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards usually treats each successful trace as a separate token sequence, so serialization choices can be mistaken for logical dependencies. We introduce Verifier-Certified Rule Transport (VCRT), which replays adjacent operation pairs with native verifiers. Pairs whose two orders are accepted and reach the same canonical state provide commutation certificates; rejected or state-changing reversals provide anti-diamonds. VCRT uses anti-diamonds to preserve genuine prerequisites and assigns policy credit to the total probability mass of each certified orbit. It also constrains post-swap consistency, source retention, and policy drift. We evaluate leave-one-environment-out transfer across ProofWriter, CLRS, and Lean through a shared anonymized relation-graph interface. All training and checkpoint decisions are frozen before held-out evaluation, which uses one greedy trajectory per item without search or verifier feedback. VCRT obtains a 77.60% macro pass rate versus 64.53% for the strongest matched baseline, a paired gain of 13.06 points (95% bootstrap CI [12.58, 13.54]). Lean accounts for most of this gain at 33.49 points, while ProofWriter and CLRS improve by 2.85 points on average. Mechanism tests consistently favor anti-diamond supervision, whereas No-Orbit is statistically indistinguishable from full VCRT. The evidence does not establish a general benefit from exact orbit aggregation.

</details>

*Xiaohe Jiang,Guoqiang Zhang,Tianjin Huang,Ronghui Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出牛顿-舒尔茨注意力（NS-Attn.），一种无参数变换，用于注意力头输出以降低谱集中度并提高有效秩。在ViT和Swin上于CIFAR-10/100数据集进行实验，最终epoch准确率一致提升0.25-0.83个百分点。谱分析显示最大特征值集中度降低，有效秩增加，但会增加推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力头输出的谱集中度高、有效秩低，限制了表示多样性和模型性能。

Method: NS-Attn. 应用于Transformer中每个注意力头的输出。具体地，每个头的输出被排列为特征×令牌的矩阵，首先按Frobenius范数归一化。然后应用有限步的牛顿-舒尔茨多项式变换，再将结果恢复原始范数。变换后的输出进行标准的头合并和输出投影。该变换旨在减少谱集中度并增加有效秩，从而改善表示质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最终epoch准确率", "value": "提升0.25-0.83个百分点", "setting": "ViT和Swin在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，12组匹配种子对比全部提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出无参数的牛顿-舒尔茨注意力（NS-Attn.）模块，在每个注意力头输出后应用有限步NS多项式变换；通过对特征-令牌矩阵进行Frobenius范数归一化与恢复，进行谱变换以降低最大特征值集中度并提高有效秩；在ViT和Swin上验证了NS-Attn.的有效性，并通过消融实验表明一次迭代优于两次

Abstract: Newton-Schulz (NS) iteration has recently been used in the Muon optimizer to transform update matrices during the training of large language models. Motivated by its spectral effect, we investigate applying NS directly to Transformer attention representations. We introduce Newton-Schulz Attention (NS-Attn.), a parameter-free transformation applied to the output of each attention head. Each head output is arranged as a feature-by-token matrix and normalized by its Frobenius norm. We then apply a finite NS polynomial step and restore the original norm. The objective is to reduce spectral concentration and increase effective rank before standard head merging and output projection. Across ViT and Swin on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, NS-Attn. improves final-epoch accuracy in all 12 matched-seed comparisons, with mean gains of 0.25--0.83 percentage points. ViT ablations show higher mean accuracy with one iteration than with two. Spectral analysis further shows reduced leading-eigenvalue concentration and increased effective rank. These gains incur additional inference latency.

</details>

*Tian Zhou,Beverly Jin,Linxiao Yang,Xue Wang,Wenwei Wang,Bingqing Peng,Mengni Ye,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究表格基础模型在上下文中的可复用计算，提出 in-situ representation refinement 与 attention-gated、FFN-free 的 RefineICL 架构，在 AMLB29、TabArena 和 TabZilla 上取得优于 TabPFN-3 的结果并通过干预实验解释支持集更新的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在每张表都定义一个新监督任务的设定下，表格基础模型应在上下文中学习什么可复用的计算，以及支持集标签如何通过非参数更新改造 episode 表示并迁移到查询样本。

Method: 方法从 in-situ representation refinement 出发：每个表格 episode 包含支持集（带标签）与查询集（无标签），支持集标签用于更新该 episode 内部的表示，更新迁移到查询而不改变模型参数。正则化 leave-one-out 目标给出两个部分：支持集修正项与其查询扩展；对目标主导项的分析把计算拆成基于注意力的读取（attention-based reading）与状态依赖的缩放（state-dependent scaling），前者负责从上下文中选择信息，后者根据当前 episode 状态调节幅度。据此实现 RefineICL：一个 attention-gated、无 FFN 的 contextual stack，用门控注意力替代前馈层，只保留选择性低秩特征交互模块，并配有 typed memory 以区分/存储不同类型的上下文状态。推理时按 episode 前向传播，支持集更新先在内部生成任务特定表示，再作用于查询，从而在上下文中构造任务特定的预测器。内部干预实验进一步验证：删除一个中间支持集更新但保留查询输出，会使最终查询交叉熵在全部 72 个 episode 上升，说明支持表示更新不是静态标签来源，而是构造预测器的关键计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "OVR-AUC", "value": "0.93836", "setting": "RefineICL-L24, AMLB29"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "0.87173", "setting": "RefineICL-L24, AMLB29"}, {"metric": "Elo", "value": "1644.8", "setting": "38-dataset TabArena snapshot, 相同评估下比 TabPFN-3 高 31.4 Elo"}, {"metric": "peak inference memory", "value": "+60.2%", "setting": "L8, 扩展 FFN 相对 RefineICL 的峰值推理内存；匹配的 100K-update depth grid 中扩展 FFN 无一致验证收益"}, {"metric": "final query cross-entropy", "value": "在全部 72 个测试 episode 中均上升", "setting": "移除一个中间支持集更新但保留查询输出"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 in-situ representation refinement 框架：支持集标签引导 episode 表示更新，更新迁移到查询而不改变模型参数；推导正则化 leave-one-out 目标，得到支持集修正项及其查询扩展；将主导项分解为 attention-based reading 与 state-dependent scaling，据此提出 RefineICL；RefineICL：attention-gated、无 FFN 的 contextual stack，结合选择性低秩特征交互与 typed memory；通过内部干预实验证明支持表示更新本身具有超越静态标签来源的作用

Abstract: What reusable computation should a tabular foundation model learn when every table defines a new supervised task? We develop in-situ representation refinement: support labels guide updates to the episode's representations, and these updates transfer to unlabeled queries without changing model parameters. A regularized leave-one-out objective yields a support correction and its query extension. The leading term separates attention-based reading from state-dependent scaling, motivating RefineICL: an attention-gated, FFN-free contextual stack with selected low-rank feature interaction and typed memory. RefineICL-L24 reaches 0.93836 OVR-AUC and 0.87173 accuracy on AMLB29. A benchmark-informed continuation reaches 1644.8 Elo on the 38-dataset TabArena snapshot, 31.4 Elo above TabPFN-3 under the same evaluation. It also improves all four reported metrics over TabPFN-v3 on both TabZilla views. In a matched 100K-update depth grid, an expanded FFN gives no consistent validation benefit and uses 60.2% more peak inference memory at L8. Internal interventions show that support representations are more than a static source of labels: removing one intermediate support update, while preserving the query output, increases final query cross-entropy in all 72 tested episodes. Together, the derivation and interventions explain how attention-gated updates can construct a task-specific predictor in context.

</details>

Conclusion: Path-Utility Unification (PUU)：在单一KL正则化目标中结合验证器奖励与教师到锚点的路径对数比，并在组归一化和PPO裁剪之前合并两种奖励，使教师证据影响响应排序。；Entropy-Calibrated Redistribution (ECR)：利用教师与旧策略的带符号token差距重新分配验证器组件，使用全词表教师熵衰减不确定指导，并通过响应级零和投影在裁剪前保持总任务信用及其token级符号。；统一在GRPO式更新中在响应级和token级同时整合验证器与教师信号，实现熵校准的信用分配。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) supervises mathematical reasoning through final-answer correctness, but provides little guidance on individual tokens. On-policy distillation (OPD) supplies dense feedback on student-generated responses, yet teacher preference need not reflect correctness. Recent hybrids combine OPD and verifier-derived advantages or reweight task credit using teacher ratios. However, teacher guidance enters after verifier-based group normalization, and token reweighting need not preserve the total task credit assigned to each response. We introduce Unified Entropy-Calibrated Credit Redistribution for GRPO (UECR-GRPO), which integrates verifier and teacher signals within a single GRPO-style update at both the response and token levels. \emph{Path-Utility Unification} (PUU) combines verifier reward and a teacher-to-anchor path log-ratio in a single KL-regularized objective. Its on-policy implementation uses a length-normalized teacher score and combines both rewards before group normalization and PPO clipping, allowing teacher evidence to influence the response ranking. \emph{Entropy-Calibrated Redistribution} (ECR) then uses the signed teacher--old-policy token gap to redistribute the verifier-derived component. Full-vocabulary teacher entropy attenuates uncertain guidance, while a response-wise zero-sum projection preserves the total task credit and its token-wise sign before clipping. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, UECR-GRPO achieves average \(\mathrm{Avg@12}\) accuracies of 17.21\% and 65.09\% with Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B students, respectively, exceeding the strongest baseline at each scale by 0.89 and 0.56 percentage points.

</details>

*Sandra Arcos-Holzinger,Debashish Chakraborty,Rohita Mocharla,Will Walden,Andrew Yates,Reno Kriz,Sarah M. Erfani,James Bailey,Vishal M. Patel,Sanjeev Khudanpur*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视频与多模态场景中，如何在不依赖任务标签和大量重复评估的情况下，对模型不同层的表示进行分层刻画，并判断哪一层最适合下游任务。

Method: LAYERSCOPE 是一个无需标签、逐层分析模型学习表示结构的框架。输入为视频与多模态模型的中间层及最终层表示；对每一层表示，框架计算四类几何度量：局部几何、全局几何、分布性度量（如 LID、RankMe）以及对应性/配对感知度量。这些度量用于比较同一模型内部不同层之间、以及不同模型之间的表示结构。评估流程不依赖任务特定标签，也不需要重复的任务特定微调，从而降低计算开销。在 MVEB/MVEB+ 基准上，对七个架构多样的模型进行视频与多模态分类、聚类以及文本到视频检索任务的下游评估，通过比较各层表示在这些任务上的表现，分析几何度量与下游性能之间的关系。核心发现包括：中间层表示可能优于最终层或默认层；没有单一几何度量能普遍预测性能；LID 与性能关系任务相关；RankMe 对分类/聚类预测最强但非通用层选择器；配对感知度量对检索的解释力优于单纯分布距离。该框架为跨模型、跨层的表示比较提供了系统化手段。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 LAYERSCOPE 无标签、分层的表示表征框架，联合使用局部、全局、分布性和对应性几何度量比较层内与跨模型的表示结构；在 MVEB/MVEB+ 的七个架构多样模型上系统评测，发现中间层表示可优于最终层和模型默认输出；发现没有单一几何度量能一致预测下游性能，但不同模型家族呈现不同的分层几何签名；发现 LID 与性能的关系依赖任务，RankMe 对分类和聚类最强但并非通用层选择器，配对感知度量比纯分布距离更能解释检索性能

Abstract: We propose LAYERSCOPE, a label-free, layerwise framework that aims to characterize a model's learned representations in video and multimodal settings. Evaluating downstream performance using representations from final or intermediate layers typically requires large amounts of labeled data, repeated task-specific evaluations, and substantial computation. To address these limitations, LAYERSCOPE uses local, global, distributional, and correspondence-based geometric metrics to compare layerwise representation structure within and across models without requiring task-specific labels. We evaluate seven architecturally diverse models across video and multimodal classification, clustering, and text-to-video retrieval tasks from MVEB/MVEB+. We find that intermediate-layer representations can outperform final-layer and model-default outputs. We also find that no single geometric metric consistently predicts downstream performance, but note that distinct layerwise geometric signatures emerge across model families. LID shows task-dependent relationships with performance, while RankMe provides the strongest measure for classification and clustering, but is not a universal layer selector. We also find that pairing-aware metrics explain retrieval better than distributional distances alone. LAYERSCOPE therefore offers a framework for comparing representations across models and layers, enabling a more systematic evaluation in video and multimodal settings.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we are concerned with matrices formed by block-diagonal factors interleaved with fixed permutations -- a flexible family of structured matrices. This class has recently drawn interest in deep learning architectures for its balanced expressivity-efficiency trade-off, yet efficient computational strategies for working with it remain to be found. We approach this problem through Riemannian geometry and examine under what conditions this class admits a smooth manifold structure. For the practically important case of orthogonal two-factor matrices, we derive the essential Riemannian tools and propose efficient algorithms for their implementation. The algorithms leverage automatic differentiation, support parameter sharing within each factor, and avoid explicit dense matrix construction. We test them within the Riemannian optimization framework on the best matrix approximation problem and for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models. Beyond the two-factor setting, we study the geometric and matrix-theoretic properties of factorizations with a larger number of block-diagonal factors.

</details>

*Lixing Li*

TL;DR: NGN提出一种可微参数化方法，通过学习边界参数来选择有序结构组件的前缀，从而实现神经网络大小的自动学习。实验表明部署学到的前缀通常性能变化很小，选出的架构与固定大小模型性能相当。该方法可应用于多种架构，包括Transformer和LoRA，为模型压缩和架构搜索提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将神经网络结构大小的选择从训练前超参数变为可微优化目标，从而在训练中直接学习有序结构组件的数量。

Method: NGN将每个有序结构组件组（如网络层、注意力头等）与一个可学习的边界参数关联。训练时，边界参数通过可微方式（如sigmoid或阶跃函数的松弛）决定哪些组件被激活，通常选择前缀。激活的组件参与前向传播，未激活的组件被跳过或置零。边界参数与模型参数一起通过梯度下降优化，边界可从较小的初始化值（即较少的组件）开始，允许在训练中增长。目标函数通常为任务损失加上对边界值的正则化或约束。推理时，直接移除边界之外的组件，得到更紧凑的模型。该方法在多种架构上验证，包括MLP、CNN、GNN、Transformer、状态空间模型、LoRA和适配器，每个架构的结构组件组对应可学习的边界。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 可微边界参数选择结构化组件组的前缀，使组件数量成为可学习的连续变量；边界可从紧凑初始化开始增长，部署时直接丢弃边界之外的组件；该机制可推广至MLP、卷积网络、图网络、Transformer、状态空间模型、LoRA和适配器等多种架构

Abstract: Neural network size is usually chosen before training, separating architecture selection from weight optimization. We introduce the Neurogenesis Network (NGN), a differentiable parameterization for learning how many ordered structural components a model should use. For each ordered component group, one learnable boundary selects an active prefix while the model parameters are trained. The boundary can grow from a compact initialization and can be deployed by discarding components beyond the learned boundary. Controlled experiments examine convergence of the learned boundary, the performance of deployed prefixes, and comparisons with fixed-size models and alternative approaches to learning capacity. We then apply the same mechanism to MLPs, convolutional and graph networks, Transformers, state-space models, LoRA, and adapters. Across these settings, deploying only the learned prefix usually changes performance little, and the selected architectures perform similarly to fixed models trained at the same size. These results show that structural capacity can be optimized directly as a count.

</details>


### [45] [Repurposing Pre-trained LLMs as High Fidelity Continuous Text Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27248)
*Arkanath Pathak,Unnat Jain,Alexander C. Berg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 LLMAE，将预训练 decoder-only LLM 改造为连续文本自编码器：在模型内部激活中引入固定长度潜在瓶颈，通过结构化注意力掩码、LoRA 适配与 KL 正则化学习自编码接口。基于 270M Gemma 3 实例化，在 1024 token 序列上实现接近完美的重建，并展示了用该潜在表示训练文本扩散模型进行图像描述的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本缺乏类似图像自编码器的高保真连续潜在表示；自回归逐 token 预测仅通过序列分解间接建模全局结构，难以获得固定长度、忠实的连续文本表征。

Method: LLMAE 以预训练的 decoder-only 语言模型 Gemma 3（270M）为基础。核心思路是：在模型内部激活中暴露一个固定长度的潜在瓶颈，使得输入文本序列被编码为该固定长度连续向量，然后解码重建原始文本。具体地，模型使用结构化注意力掩码来控制信息流动，确保潜在瓶颈位置只能聚合全局信息而不能直接看到未来 token；同时通过 LoRA 对原 LLM 参数进行参数高效适配，并施加 KL 正则化将潜在表示约束到合适的分布。训练目标为自编码重建损失（可能为交叉熵或类似损失）与 KL 正则项的联合优化。推理时，输入文本经过 LLM 前端编码为固定长度潜向量，再由解码部分重建文本；该潜向量也可作为下游任务（如文本扩散模型的条件输入）的表示，用于图像描述生成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "重建保真度", "value": "接近完美重建（论文未给出具体指标数值）", "setting": "序列长度最多 1024 token，基于 270M Gemma 3 模型"}, {"metric": "图像描述生成", "value": "通过潜在文本扩散模型完成详细图像描述（论文未给出具体指标数值）", "setting": "使用 LLMAE 学到的连续潜在表示"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在预训练解码器-only LLM 内部激活中插入固定长度潜在瓶颈，将 LLM 改造为连续文本自编码器。；采用结构化注意力掩码、LoRA 参数高效适配与 KL 正则化训练自编码接口，复用原 LLM 的生成先验。；在潜在空间上训练文本扩散模型用于详细图像描述，验证所学连续文本表示的实用性。

Abstract: Next-token prediction has enabled highly fluent autoregressive language models, but it represents global structure only indirectly through sequential factorization. In contrast, high-fidelity autoencoders have become a standard primitive in image generation, enabling generative models to operate over continuous latent spaces; text lacks a comparably faithful continuous representation. We propose LLMAE, a method for repurposing a pretrained decoder-only language model as a continuous text autoencoder by exposing an intermediate fixed-length latent bottleneck within its internal activations. Instantiated with a parameter-efficient 270M Gemma 3 model, LLMAE uses structured attention masks, LoRA adaptation, and KL regularization to learn an autoencoding interface that leverages the generative prior of the original LLM. We train LLMAE to reconstruct text sequences up to 1024 tokens, significantly improving on this task to achieve near-perfect reconstruction. Furthermore, we demonstrate the downstream utility of this representation by training a latent text diffusion model for detailed image captioning using the learned LLMAE autoencoder. By mapping text into a fixed-length continuous latent space, our approach provides an effective substrate for downstream adaptation while benefiting from the fluency of the original LLM.

</details>


### [46] [FLEET: From Logits Entropy to Enhanced Trajectories in Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27657)
*Oleksii Streltsov,Oleksandra Vitko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLEET 在 LLM 生成中引入基于高熵稀疏轨迹的记忆机制，用 per-token 效用分数调整 logits，以缓解重复采样带来的语义重复和收益递减。基准评测中与重复采样基线同精度下实现 3x 加速，LiveCodeBench Pass@32 从 59.9% 提升到 66.2%。方法对现有 LLM 流水线改动小，一次校准即可获得主要超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度采样等无记忆的重复采样方法在增加采样量时会产生大量语义重复答案，带来递减收益，且浪费推理预算。FLEET 试图在生成过程中引入记忆机制，用历史高熵轨迹带来的效用信号调整 logits，从而提高采样效率与困难任务准确率。

Method: FLEET 面向 LLM 的生成/解码过程。输入是语言模型在自回归解码中产生的 logits 分布；关键在于识别熵超过预设阈值的高熵状态，并将一次生成记录为这些高熵状态的稀疏序列，即“轨迹”。该轨迹作为记忆，使后续采样不再完全无记忆：系统利用历史轨迹推断每个 token 的效用分数，再用这些 per-token 效用分数调整当前/后续 logits，从而引导生成避开语义重复方向并提升困难任务上的有效采样比例。论文评估的配置包含贪婪解码模式，在该模式下方法被报告为确定性；主超参数通过一次校准 pass 得到。整体流程是对现有 LLM 生成流水线的最小侵入式修改：在解码过程中维护轨迹记忆、计算效用并调整 logits，然后继续采样。论文未披露具体的模型规模、训练数据、硬件或线上服务细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LiveCodeBench Pass@32", "value": "从 59.9% 提升到 66.2%", "setting": "复杂编码任务，相同预算，与重复采样基线对比"}, {"metric": "准确率与推理速度", "value": "达到与重复采样基线相同准确率，3x 加速", "setting": "基准评测，与重复采样基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每次生成表示为经过熵超过阈值的状态的稀疏轨迹，用轨迹作为记忆表示；基于稀疏轨迹推断 per-token 效用分数，并用其调整 logits 以影响后续生成；在论文评估的贪婪解码配置下方法确定，且主超参数仅需一次校准 pass 即可导出；对现有 LLM 流水线只需最小修改即可接入

Abstract: Solutions based on large language models (LLMs) often rely on temperature sampling to improve accuracy and stability by aggregating multiple samples from the completion distribution. However, this memoryless approach is inherently suboptimal: because it lacks awareness of prior generations and their evaluations, it produces an increasing proportion of semantically duplicate answers as more samples are drawn, leading to diminishing returns. To address this limitation, we introduce FLEET, a novel method that integrates a memory mechanism into the generation process. FLEET represents each generation as a sparse trajectory through states whose entropy exceeds a predefined threshold and uses these trajectories to infer per-token utility scores that adjust the logits. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate that FLEET achieves the same accuracy as the repeated sampling baseline, with a 3x speedup, and substantially improves accuracy on complex coding tasks (LiveCodeBench Pass@32 increases from 59.9% to 66.2%) under the same budget. Furthermore, in the greedy-decoding configuration evaluated here, the approach is deterministic and uses a single calibration pass to derive its principal hyperparameters, requiring only minimal modifications to existing LLM pipelines.

</details>

Motivation: 现有基于规则或奖励模型的RL reward system存在优化不稳定与奖励劫持（reward hacking）问题，论文将其归结为策略流形与奖励流形在训练过程中的不匹配（policy-reward manifold mismatch）。

Method: DCRL将LLM推理视为由逻辑演绎（logical deduction）、评估（evaluation）与表示（representation）三个相互依赖子流形耦合而成的一般流形。响应生成被解释为从评估流形解耦的过程，奖励估计被解释为从逻辑演绎流形解耦的过程；规则式与奖励模型式RL系统的缺陷被几何地解释为训练过程中策略流形与奖励流形的不匹配。框架包含两个组件：(1) 基于三段论逻辑的提示演化机制，动态精炼奖励评分标准（reward rubrics），从而增强奖励流形的表达能力；(2) 策略-奖励重耦合机制，联合更新奖励模型与策略模型，确保评估一致性并缓解流形不匹配。训练目标为在RL过程中对齐策略流形与奖励流形，以稳定优化并抑制奖励劫持；推理阶段使用经DCRL训练后的策略模型直接生成推理响应。摘要未披露更多输入表示、具体网络模块或服务部署细节。

Conclusion: 从几何视角将LLM通用推理建模为逻辑演绎、评估与表示三个相互依赖子流形构成的耦合流形，并把响应生成解释为从评估流形解耦、奖励估计解释为从逻辑演绎流形解耦。；提出基于三段论逻辑的提示演化机制（syllogistic logic-based prompt evolution），动态精炼奖励评分标准（reward rubrics），以增强奖励流形的表达能力。；提出策略-奖励重耦合机制（policy-reward re-coupling），联合更新奖励模型与策略模型，保证评估一致性并缓解训练中的流形不匹配。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing reward systems, such as rule-based and reward-model-based, often exhibit issues such as unstable optimization and reward hacking. In this work, we revisit the general reasoning of LLMs from a geometric perspective, conceptualizing it as a coupled manifold composed of three interdependent sub-manifolds: logical deduction, evaluation, and representation. Based on this perspective, response generation in RL can be interpreted as a decoupling process from the evaluation manifold, while reward estimation corresponds to a decoupling process from the logical deduction manifold. The limitations of rule-based and reward-model RL systems can be geometrically interpreted as the mismatch of policy-reward manifolds during RL process. To address the aforementioned misalignment, we propose Decoupling and Coupling Reinforcement Learning (DCRL) framework, which incorporates two key components: (1) a syllogistic logic-based prompt evolution mechanism that dynamically refines reward rubrics to enhance the expressiveness of the reward manifold; and (2) a policy-reward re-coupling mechanism that jointly updates the reward and policy models, ensuring consistent evaluation and mitigating manifold mismatch during training. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments across multiple reasoning domains demonstrate that DCRL consistently outperforms both rule-based and reward-model baselines. Notably, a Qwen3-4B model trained under DCRL surpasses a Qwen3-32B baseline and approaches the performance of a Qwen3-235B model, highlighting superior effectiveness and generalization in RL.

</details>

Abstract: Sequential RecSys are central to modern personalization, exploiting user's historical interaction sequences to drive next-step decisions. Deep learning models, particularly CNN and Transformer-based architectures, have proven highly effective at capturing temporal dependencies in these histories. For transparency and trust, understanding which past interactions drive a given recommendation is increasingly important --- both for developers auditing model behavior and for users seeking a rationale. However, the non-linearities that give these models their predictive power also render them black boxes, making it difficult to attribute decisions to specific interactions. While gradient-based, perturbation-based, and attention-based explainability methods exist, a systematic benchmark of their faithfulness for sequential recommendation is missing. We address this gap by introducing a dual-model masking metric in which one model supplies per-timestep attribution scores and a separately trained, masking-robust probe measures the resulting change in predicted probability. Using this metric, we benchmark ten XAI methods across CNN, Transformer, SASRec, and BERT4Rec backbones on KuaiRand and MovieLens, complemented by analyses of temporal attribution patterns, item popularity confounding, and robustness to input corruption. Our key findings are: (1) gradient-based methods, particularly GradientSHAP and Integrated Gradients, yield the most faithful and robust attributions; (2) raw attention weights are unreliable, but gradient-weighted attention restores faithfulness on shorter sequences, with degradation on longer horizons as softmax attention probabilities converge toward uniform importance scores, diminishing the method's ability to identify informative interactions; and (3) temporal attribution patterns in faithful methods reflect genuine task structure rather than recency or popularity bias.

</details>

*Abbas Mammadov,Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Partha Kaushik,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Yee Whye Teh,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WTF提出基于Wasserstein倾斜的无模拟强化学习微调方法，用于流映射生成模型。通过最优传输正则化替代KL倾斜，将样本向高奖励区域传输，并转化为确定性最优控制问题实现无需模拟的端到端训练。在ImageNet-256和文生图上，以最高280倍更少的训练计算量取得更高奖励和相当或更高多样性，无需事后蒸馏即可少步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何对预训练的流匹配生成模型进行奖励微调，以提升下游奖励，同时避免KL正则化带来的分布重加权限制，并保持少步推理性能。

Method: WTF框架基于预训练的流映射模型。输入为噪声样本或条件（如文本），通过预训练流映射生成样本。核心修改在于微调目标：用基于预训练漂移的最优传输正则化替换传统KL正则化，使得微调后的流映射将个体样本沿高奖励方向传输，而非重新加权基础分布。该目标被证明等价于一个确定性最优控制问题，其中控制量作用于流轨迹，奖励作为终端代价。利用此等价性，算法无需在训练中模拟完整生成过程即可计算梯度，实现无模拟强化学习。微调后的流映射可直接用于少步采样，无需额外的蒸馏步骤。训练目标为最大化期望奖励减去Wasserstein距离惩罚，优化通过随机梯度下降类方法进行。推理时，使用微调后的流映射以少步数生成样本，保持奖励对齐和高多样性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练计算量减少", "value": "最高280倍", "setting": "ImageNet-256和文生图实验，与基线方法比较"}, {"metric": "奖励", "value": "更高", "setting": "ImageNet-256和文生图，与基线方法比较"}, {"metric": "多样性", "value": "相当或更高", "setting": "ImageNet-256和文生图，与基线方法比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于预训练漂移的最优传输正则化，将奖励倾斜建模为Wasserstein倾斜而非KL倾斜，实现样本向高奖励区域传输而非重加权。；证明该问题等价于流量上的确定性最优控制问题，从而给出无模拟的强化学习算法，可直接微调流映射。；首个对流映射端到端微调的配方，无需事后蒸馏即可在少步推理预算下保持奖励对齐性能。；实验表明相比基线，在ImageNet-256和文生图任务上达到更高奖励并保持相当或更高多样性，训练计算量减少最高280倍。

Abstract: Reward fine-tuning aims to update a pre-trained flow-based generative model to improve the downstream reward of its generated samples. Existing methods typically formulate this problem as sampling from a reward-tilted distribution, the solution to a KL-regularized reward-maximization problem. Here, we introduce an optimal transport regularizer built directly from the pre-trained drift. Unlike KL reward tilting, the resulting objective transports individual samples toward higher reward rather than reweighting the base distribution. We show that the resulting problem is equivalent to a deterministic optimal control problem on the flow. Given a pre-trained flow map, this equivalence yields a simulation-free reinforcement learning algorithm for fine-tuning generative flows. We call the resulting framework Wasserstein-Tilted Flow Maps (WTF), the first end-to-end fine-tuning recipe native to flow maps. The output is a fine-tuned flow map that retains strong reward-aligned performance at few-step inference budgets without post-hoc distillation. Experiments on ImageNet-256 and text-to-image show that WTF achieves higher reward with comparable or higher diversity than baselines, while requiring up to $280\times$ less training compute. More broadly, we argue that accelerated samplers such as flow maps are essential infrastructure for efficient post-training, and that the dominant KL-regularized formulation is only one of many choices worth revisiting.

</details>

*Hardhik Mohanty,Indrayana Rustandi,Mohamadreza Sheibani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP是一种面向大规模不平衡表格数据的线性时间复杂度核心集剪枝方法，通过代理模型分数分层分配负类预算并加权校正，在95%负类削减下保留生产欺诈数据集99.7%的平均精度，大幅降低GBDT训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模不平衡表格数据集导致重复的梯度提升树训练成本高昂，现有核心集方法在去除大量多数类样本时往往损失精度。

Method: CRISP包含以下阶段：1）使用一个代理模型（proxy model）为训练样本打分；2）根据代理模型分数将负类样本划分到多个分位数层（quantile strata）；3）在每个层内分配负类预算，预算分配策略为各层分配不同比例的负类样本保留额度；4）在预算内采样负类样本，并为每个保留样本计算样本权重以校正不等包含概率（逆倾向加权）；5）使用加权的核心集训练梯度提升树（GBDT）模型。最终推理时仅使用训练好的GBDT模型进行预测，无需代理模型或核心集机制。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Average Precision", "value": "保留99.7%全量数据AP", "setting": "生产欺诈数据集，95%负类削减，训练行数从25M降至约1.70M（减少93.2%）"}, {"metric": "平均Average Precision", "value": "在90%至99.4%多数类削减率下均为最高均值AP", "setting": "CriteoPrivateAds公开数据集"}, {"metric": "平均Average Precision", "value": "在99.2%和99.4%多数类削减率下均为最高均值AP", "setting": "Sparkov数据集（较低削减率下结果混合）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于代理模型分数的分位数分层负类预算分配策略；样本权重校正不等包含概率（逆倾向加权）；线性时间复杂度的核心集构建方法

Abstract: Large imbalanced tabular datasets make repeated gradient-boosted tree training expensive. Existing coreset methods often lose accuracy when most majority examples are removed. We present CRISP (Coreset Reduction via Importance-Stratified Pruning), a linear-time method that allocates a negative-class budget across quantile strata of a proxy-model score. Sample weights account for unequal inclusion probabilities. At 95% negative-class reduction on a production fraud dataset, CRISP trains on approximately 1.70M of 25M rows and retains 99.7% of full-data Average Precision. This is a 93.2% reduction in total training rows. On public CriteoPrivateAds, CRISP has the highest mean Average Precision at each tested rate from 90% to 99.4% majority reduction. Sparkov results are mixed at lower rates, but CRISP has the highest mean at 99.2% and 99.4%. Ablations identify budget allocation and inverse-propensity weighting as the main sources of the production-dataset gain.

*Jialu Wang,Jianing Deng,Shuqing Luo,Yuanzhe Li,Dongwei Wang,Jingtong Hu,Huanrui Yang,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

TL;DR: 该论文针对 LLM 遗忘在量化后失效的问题，从损失景观交互角度提出基于曲率的敏感权重判据，并设计敏感性引导噪声正则化与遗忘关键优化两个模块，在 MUSE 与 TOFU 基准上实现更抗量化的遗忘且保持效用。

Abstract: Unlearning ensures LLM compliance by removing the influence of private or copyrighted training data. However, since LLM models typically undergo post-training compression, like quantization, in practical deployment, it has been observed that the unlearning effect can be substantially weakened, with the forgetting behavior degrading more severely than that of model utility. This paper proposes a quantization-robust unlearning framework that makes forgetting robust to quantization while maintaining overall model utility. We analyze this gap through the lens of loss landscape. Specifically, our analysis reveals a curvature-based criteria that pinpoints sensitive weights in the unlearned model that leads to both non-robust forgetting and reduced utility. We therefore propose sensitivity-guided noisy regularization, which is applied on the sensitive parameters to steer the model convergence towards a smoother minima of uniformly low forget and retain losses. Balancing unlearning and utility, we further propose forget-critical optimization, which updates only forget-critical layers, preserving most of the network to retain useful knowledge. Extensive experiments on the MUSE and TOFU benchmarks across multiple LLM unlearning algorithms show that our approach achieves substantially more quantization-resilient forgetting while maintaining utility.

</details>


### [52] [KITE: KV-Invariant Transformer Expansion for Efficient Agentic LLM Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27294)
*Zhiheng Hu,Yixun Wei,Jian Zhou,Yizhuang Zhou,Ji Li,Xing Chen,Yang Li,Bojun Wang,Yibo Zhu,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KITE提出一种KV不变的Transformer扩展范式，采用双塔SST解码器，在upcycling时将新参数置于不影响KV的区域，推理时预填充仅依赖小塔，从而降低训练与推理成本。67B MoE模型在可比训练计算量下损失低于47B和63B MoE，估计推理成本分别降低6.7%和31.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在降低训练、预填充和自回归解码计算成本的同时，保证更大模型的质量优于更小基线，从而高效扩展语言模型。

Method: KITE扩展范式将模型从小尺寸扩展到大尺寸（upcycling），新增的参数放置在不会影响注意力KV计算的区域。具体实例化SST为两塔解码器：一塔（较小部分）负责产生KV，另一塔（新增较大参数部分）读取KV。推理时，预填充阶段仅依赖产生KV的较小塔，因此预填充KV的计算量减少；解码阶段新增参数参与读取但不改变KV，使得推理成本低于同等规模的传统MoE Transformer。训练目标为语言建模损失，通过扩展训练在可比累积训练计算量下达到更低损失。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "training loss", "value": "低于47B MoE (1.48B active) 和63B MoE (2.02B active)", "setting": "67B MoE (2.15B active body parameters per decode token) 在可比累积训练计算量下"}, {"metric": "estimated inference cost reduction", "value": "6.7% vs 47B MoE, 31.6% vs 63B MoE", "setting": "与对应47B/63B MoE Transformer对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出KV-Invariant Transformer Expansion (KITE) 扩展范式：从较小模型扩展至较大模型（upcycling），新增参数被置于不影响注意力KV的区域。；提出Step Scale Transformer (SST) 双塔解码器：一塔生成KV，另一塔读取KV；推理时预填KV仅依赖较小部分模型，节省推理成本。

Abstract: Scaling a language model is not only a question of final quality: the architectural choice determines how much computation is spent during training, prompt processing, and autoregressive decoding to achieve certain model quality. An ideal model architecture should lower all above computation costs to facilitate scaling to a larger model, while ensure the larger model indeed outperforms smaller baselines. We introduce KV-Invariant Transformer Expansion (KITE), a scaling paradigm that achieves this goal. It trains the model from a smaller size to a larger size (i.e., saving training costs via upcycling), while places newly added parameters in regions that do not affect attention KV. Consequently, during inference, prefilling KV only relies on the smaller part of the model, so the inference costs are saved. As a concrete instantiation, we present Step Scale Transformer (SST), a two-tower decoder in which one tower produces KV and the other reads them. At comparable cumulative training compute, SST, a 67B MoE model with 2.15B active body parameters per decode token, achieves lower training loss than 47B and 63B MoE Transformers with 1.48B and 2.02B active body parameters, respectively, while reducing estimated inference cost by 6.7% and 31.6%.

</details>


### [53] [COPE: Continual Personalization of LLMs under Sparse User Feedback via User Embeddings and Self-Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26853)
*Ruike Cao,Fugen Yao,Liang Dong,Jian Xu,Guanjun Jiang,Li Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COPE提出一种面向稀疏用户反馈的LLM持续个性化框架，为每个用户学习嵌入向量，并通过自评估生成代理奖励，在单步更新中联合优化偏好捕获、自评估校准与个性化响应。实验表明其在稀疏反馈下优于强基线，且与RAP互补，能保持通用能力并应对偏好漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏用户反馈的真实交互场景中，如何对LLM进行持续个性化优化，同时避免占用上下文窗口的prompt工程，并克服静态后训练无法持续适应的问题。

Method: COPE为每个用户维护一个可学习的个性化嵌入向量，作为用户偏好表征。在每次更新步骤中，该嵌入与输入提示结合送入LLM生成响应；模型利用自评估机制对生成响应进行质量打分，生成代理奖励信号；该奖励信号同时用于校准自评估器和优化个性化嵌入及LLM响应生成，形成偏好捕获、自评估校准与个性化响应优化的联合单步更新。训练目标为最大化代理奖励。推理时，使用更新后的用户嵌入引导LLM生成个性化响应，无需在上下文中添加额外提示。方法可与检索增强提示（RAP）结合使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 为用户分配可学习个性化嵌入，并在单步更新中联合优化；利用自评估生成代理奖励，在无显式反馈时实现持续更新；将偏好捕获、自评估校准与个性化响应优化整合到统一框架

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable results across various benchmarks, their alignment with normative values often results in homogenized responses that fail to address diverse user preferences. Existing training-free methods often occupy valuable context windows through prompt engineering, while training-based methods typically remain static post-training, failing to support the continual optimization required in real-world settings. To address these challenges, we propose COPE (Continual Optimization with Personalized embedding and self-Evaluation), a novel optimization framework tailored for real-world-motivated interaction settings with sparse user feedback. Our framework assigns learnable personalized embeddings to each user and synergistically integrates preference capture, self-evaluation calibration, and personalized response optimization within a single update step. A key innovation of our method is the use of self-evaluation to generate proxy rewards, enabling continuous model updates even when explicit user feedback is unavailable. Experiments show that COPE consistently outperforms strong training-free and training-based baselines under sparse feedback, and remains complementary to Retrieval-Augmented Prompting (RAP). Further analyses confirm COPE's reliable self-evaluation, meaningful preference patterns, stable general capabilities, and robustness under shifting preferences and alternative evaluators.

</details>


### [54] [Attention-based representations for multi-task computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04243)
*Daniel Hsu,Mingyue Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论角度研究多头注意力层表示多任务所需的最小注意力头数，针对‘同时计算最小/最大值’和‘计算n位XOR’两个场景给出紧致下界与构造，并推广到对称布尔函数。对推荐系统算法工程师理解注意力机制在LLM中的表达容量与多任务泛化有基础参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力层产生的向量表示需支持多个下游任务，但所需注意力头数（或等价嵌入维度/精度）的理论下界尚不明确。

Method: 论文从理论角度分析多头注意力层的表示能力。输入为向量表示（由注意力层产生），下游任务通过线性预测器或多项式阈值函数（PTF）实现。在第一个任务场景中，需要构造一个向量表示使得线性预测器能同时计算输入列表的最小值和最大值；论文证明单个注意力头需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或位精度才能完成，而两个注意力头在较小的嵌入维度与位精度下即可完成。在第二个任务场景中，需要向量表示使多项式阈值函数能计算n位XOR；论文证明注意力头数与PTF次数的乘积至少为n，并构造多头注意力层达到该下界。这些结果进一步推广到任意对称布尔函数，下界由阈值度给出。论文的核心是理论上下界证明与构造性匹配，不涉及实际训练或推理流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明在同时计算列表中最小值和最大值的任务中，单头注意力需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或精度，而双头注意力在低维低精度下即可实现。；证明n位XOR任务中，注意力头数与多项式阈值函数次数的乘积至少为n，并构造出达到该下界的多头注意力层。；将结果推广到任意对称布尔函数，用阈值度给出下界。

Abstract: Multi-head attention layers produce vector representations that support multiple downstream tasks. We establish bounds on the number of heads required in two simple and concrete multi-task scenarios. In the first scenario, a vector representation is sought so that linear predictors can compute both the smallest and largest numbers in a given list. In this case, it is known two attention heads with small embedding dimension and bit precision level suffice. We prove that a single attention head requires exponentially higher embedding dimension or precision level. In the second scenario, a vector representation is sought so that a polynomial threshold function can compute the XOR of a given string of $n$ bits. This scenario is analogous to the first one for $n=2$, since XOR is readily computed by a linear function using a vector representation that encodes both the AND and the OR of the two bits. We observe that $n$-bit XOR requires the product of the number of heads and the polynomial degree to be at least $n$, and we construct multi-head attention layers that match this lower bound. These results generalize to arbitrary (symmetric) Boolean functions, where the bound is given in terms of the threshold degree.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
