### [66] [Large Knowledge Model: From Papers to a Scientific Reasoning Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27297)
*Yuan Huang,Sihan Hu,Hongyu Gu,Chao Ma,Jiaxing Zhang,Zhiyong Zou,Caiyu Fan,Yan Xiao,Mingjun Xu,Chenyu Xie,Mingzhen Ju,Zhehao Ma,Qi Zhang,Baozong Wang,Yu Li,Zhiyuan Yao,Ruoxue Liao,Xinyu Li,Linfeng Zhang,Kun Chen,Weinan E*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LKM将科学文献构建为源锚定的推理图，统一结构遍历与语义检索，形成问题、工作流、证据三视图，支持推理感知搜索与证据锚定问答。固定回答模型时，LKM检索在ChemBench、PubMedQA、SciBench上分别提升9.30%、4.20%、14.69%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将海量科学文献转化为共享的、计算可访问的推理资源，使研究者与智能体能够基于科学意图检索、合成可检查的论证并规划研究。

Method: LKM将科学文献转化为源锚定的推理图，图中节点关联研究问题、科学流程、结论与证据。系统在同一组对象上同时支持结构遍历和语义检索，使检索能够基于科学意图而非仅关键词。跨论文对齐相关问题、主张和推理链，形成统一的科学推理景观，并呈现为问题景观、工作流景观、证据景观三个连通视图。该统一基底支持推理感知科学搜索、证据锚定的问答、比较证据分析和研究规划。评价中固定回答模型，仅改变检索组件，在ChemBench、PubMedQA和SciBench上分别提升9.30%、4.20%和14.69%准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "+9.30%", "setting": "ChemBench，固定回答模型，LKM检索对比基线"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "+4.20%", "setting": "PubMedQA，固定回答模型，LKM检索对比基线"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "+14.69%", "setting": "SciBench，固定回答模型，LKM检索对比基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将论文表示为源锚定的推理图，连接研究问题、科学流程、结论与证据；在同一对象上耦合结构遍历与语义检索，实现推理感知的科学搜索；对齐跨论文的相关问题、主张与推理链，形成科学推理景观；构建问题景观、工作流景观、证据景观三个连通视图

Abstract: Accumulated scientific knowledge advances inquiry when prior findings help researchers choose new questions, design investigations, and interpret results. Realizing this value at scale requires access to the reasoning that connects research problems, scientific procedures, conclusions, and evidence. We introduce the Large Knowledge Model (LKM), a scientific knowledge infrastructure that transforms the literature into a shared, computationally accessible reasoning resource. LKM represents papers as source-grounded reasoning graphs, couples structural traversal with semantic retrieval over the same objects, and aligns related questions, claims, and reasoning chains across papers. This representation forms a Scientific Reasoning Landscape with three connected views: a Question Landscape that organizes research problems and open directions, a Workflow Landscape that exposes reusable scientific procedures, and an Evidence Landscape that connects conclusions to their support, disagreement, and conditions. The unified substrate supports reasoning-aware scientific search, evidence-grounded question answering, comparative evidence analysis, and research planning. Researchers and agents can retrieve relevant work through its scientific intent, synthesize answers with inspectable supporting arguments, and develop research plans informed by established workflows and unresolved evidence. We describe a corpus-scale system and evaluate scientific retrieval and knowledge-intensive question answering. With the answering model fixed, LKM retrieval improves accuracy by 9.30%, 4.20%, and 14.69% on ChemBench, PubMedQA, and SciBench, respectively. By connecting knowledge access to scientific reasoning and action, LKM provides a common foundation for discovering relevant research, reusing scientific knowledge, and coordinating cumulative inquiry across researchers, agents, and research cycles.

</details>


### [67] [Memory Control Signals Emerge Before Action in Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27286)
*Mingxuan Wang,Guorun Yao,Fei Luo,Yinglong Guo,Chao Ning,Bo Wang,Hongyue Chen,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现长时程智能体在行动前隐藏状态已编码记忆压缩与召回信号，据此提出PaMER和PaMER+框架，结合状态引导压缩与检索，并在WorkBuddyBench上验证可降低上下文消耗且保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程语言模型智能体持续积累交互历史导致计算成本增加，且相关信息的保存与复用困难；现有上下文管理方法主要关注压缩或检索历史，但未探究模型自身是否在行动前已表征记忆操作需求。

Method: PaMER包含两个核心模块：1）状态引导压缩：在每次智能体行动之前，模型隐藏状态中已含有压缩需求信号，该信号被用来决定何时以及如何压缩历史上下文，以减少上下文消耗同时保留关键信息；2）外部证据检索：当隐藏状态表征出召回需求时，系统从外部存储中检索历史证据，补充近期压缩上下文遗漏的长程依赖。PaMER+在此基础上增加步骤级证据选择模块，根据当前任务动态选择仅需的历史信息进行恢复。整体流程为：智能体与环境交互累积历史，每步行动前提取隐藏状态，解码压缩或召回信号，执行相应记忆操作，更新上下文后生成动作。训练目标未在摘要中明确，推理时依赖预训练模型自身的表征能力，无额外训练开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现智能体行动前隐藏状态已编码压缩与召回需求信号，且不能由简单上下文长度或交互进度解释，并在不同模型深度呈现不同形成模式。；提出PaMER，结合状态引导压缩与外部证据检索，利用预行动记忆信号指导记忆操作。；提出PaMER+，引入步骤级证据选择，仅恢复当前任务所需的历史信息。；构建WorkBuddyBench基准用于评估上下文管理方法。

Abstract: Long horizon language model agents continuously accumulate interaction history, increasing computational cost while making relevant information harder to preserve and reuse. Existing context management methods mainly focus on how to compress or retrieve history, but largely leave open whether the model itself already represents the need for these memory operations before they occur. We study the hidden state immediately before each agent action and find that compression and recall needs are already encoded in the model's internal representations. These signals cannot be explained by simple context length or interaction progress, and they exhibit distinct formation patterns across model depth. We further show that most memory decision information is preserved in a compact recent context, while selectively restored historical evidence complements the long range dependencies that recent context misses. Based on these findings, we propose Preaction Memory with Evidence Retrieval (PaMER), which combines state guided compression with external evidence retrieval. PaMER+ further introduces step level evidence selection to recover only the historical information required by the current task. Experiments on WorkBuddyBench, across multiple context management baselines and model backbones, show that our framework substantially reduces context consumption while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [68] [StateComp: Learning When to Compress History in Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27298)
*Mingxuan Wang,Hongyue Chen,Yinglong Guo,Fei Luo,Chao Ning,Bo Wang,Guorun Yao,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateComp提出状态条件压缩框架，动态判断长程Agent历史交互能否安全压缩，通过两阶段标注训练路由器，在WorkBuddyBench上实现token减少52.27%且性能保持，表征提取加速12.67倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程Agent执行过程中，如何根据Agent状态动态判断何时可以安全地压缩历史交互，以平衡信息保留与上下文开销。

Method: StateComp框架：输入为Agent交互历史序列。首先通过两阶段标注过程为历史交互打上KEEP（保留）或READY（可压缩）标签。然后从冻结语言模型提取隐藏表征，并训练一个不平衡感知路由器，该路由器根据当前Agent状态决定每个历史交互是否可安全压缩。为降低评估长历史的计算开销，使用有界状态表示。在执行时，相邻的READY交互被分组为连续片段，替换为紧凑摘要，从而减少上下文长度。整个流程在保持任务性能的同时减少token消耗并加速表征提取。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "总Agent与摘要token减少百分比", "value": "52.27%", "setting": "WorkBuddyBench基准测试，保持任务性能"}, {"metric": "表征提取速度提升倍数", "value": "12.67倍", "setting": "WorkBuddyBench基准测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于Agent状态的条件压缩：利用当前隐藏状态决定历史交互是否可安全替换。；两阶段标注流程构建KEEP和READY监督信号。；在冻结语言模型的隐藏表征上训练不平衡感知路由器。；有界状态表示降低评估长历史的成本。；将相邻READY交互分组为连续片段并替换为紧凑摘要。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents continuously accumulate interaction history during task execution, yet the importance of past interactions changes as the agent state evolves. Existing context management methods largely compress history based on fixed windows, periodic schedules, or current relevance, overlooking a more fundamental question: when has a past interaction become safe to replace? Premature compression may remove information still needed for future actions, while overly conservative retention leads to substantial context overhead. To address this, we propose State Conditioned Compression (StateComp), a framework that determines when historical interactions can be safely compressed according to the current agent state. StateComp constructs KEEP and READY supervision through a two-stage annotation procedure and trains an imbalance-aware router on hidden representations from a frozen language model. A bounded state representation further reduces the cost of evaluating long histories, while adjacent READY interactions are grouped into continuous spans and replaced with compact summaries during execution. Experiments on WorkBuddyBench show that StateComp reduces total agent and summarization tokens by 52.27% while maintaining task performance, and achieves a 12.67-fold speedup in representation extraction.

</details>


### [69] [PASTABench: Proactive Assessment of Sequential Trajectories for Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28197)
*Jiapeng Sun,Yujin Zhou,Han Zhu,Pengcheng Wen,Jiayi Zhou,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASTABench提出多轮LLM智能体主动安全监控基准，包含1139条带风险标注的轨迹，并定义最优干预窗口指标。评测16个模型后发现最佳模型仅40.74%在最优时机干预，且小模型依赖危险关键词的浅层模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮安全评估要么按步孤立判断而忽略风险累积，要么在轨迹结束后事后评估而无法及时干预；如何形式化并量化“是否需要干预、何时干预、风险是什么”的解耦式主动安全监控。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是构建了评测框架与基准。输入为多轮智能体交互轨迹（每条轨迹包含若干轮次的状态变化与对话/动作）。处理流程分两个阶段：离线标注阶段，对每条轨迹标注风险类别（5大类13子类）、最早信号轮（Earliest-Signal）与触发轮（Trigger），由此定义最优干预窗口（OIW）；评测阶段，将轨迹按轮输入给被测LLM，要求模型在每一轮判断是否干预、何时干预以及风险类型，并通过与标注的OIW对比计算及时性指标。利用该框架对16个LLM进行实验，并设计词汇中性化扰动以诊断模型是否依赖危险关键词而非真正理解风险。训练目标与推理服务不在论文范围。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最优时机干预率", "value": "40.74%", "setting": "最佳模型在PASTABench上的评测结果（16个LLM对比）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化Decoupled Proactive Safety Monitoring，将安全监控拆解为是否干预、何时干预、风险类别三个维度；构建PASTABench基准，包含1139条多轮轨迹，覆盖5个风险大类、13个子类，并带有风险标注；提出最优干预窗口（OIW）指标，以标注的最早信号轮和触发轮为锚点量化干预及时性；通过16个LLM的评测揭示了词汇过拟合现象：中性化危险词后模型的主动安全能力大幅崩溃

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents that alter real-world states, ensuring operational safety across multi-step workflows has become a critical challenge. While recent work has moved beyond single-turn evaluation toward multi-turn paradigms, key limitations persist: step-level methods treat actions in isolation, missing how risks accumulate, while trajectory-level evaluations operate post-hoc, offering no opportunity for timely intervention. To address these limitations, we formalize Decoupled Proactive Safety Monitoring along three dimensions: whether to intervene, when to intervene, and what the risk is. We introduce PASTABench, a benchmark of 1,139 multi-turn trajectories spanning 5 risk categories and 13 subcategories. We further propose the Optimal Intervention Window (OIW), anchored by annotated Earliest-Signal and Trigger turns, to quantify intervention timeliness. Evaluation of 16 LLMs reveals that proactive intervention remains largely unsolved, with the best model achieving only 40.74% optimal-timing interventions. Fine-grained diagnosis further uncovers pervasive lexical overfitting: competitive safety scores of smaller models mask keyword hypersensitivity rather than genuine risk comprehension, as their proactive capability largely collapses once hazard vocabulary is neutralized.

</details>


### [70] [An Open Pipeline and Dashboard for Systemic-Risk Evidence under the EU AI Act's Code of Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28335)
*Jacob T. Emmerson,Phuong-Anh Nguyen-Le,Ronan Romano,Wilber Sean V. Anterola,Yann Billeter,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Systemic Risk Index——一个开源LLM系统风险评测流水线与仪表盘，将19个公开基准组织为EU GPAI准则的四类系统风险，使用保害扰动与模拟部署情境评测18个模型，并支持平均/最坏情况聚合切换与逐项证据追溯；LLM法官与人类一致性κ=0.78-0.82，盲审显示83%扰动保持原始危害，最坏情况聚合使分数下降14-37分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将分散、不透明且静态的AI安全/系统风险主张，转化为公开可追溯、可复现经验证据的评测流水线，使风险评分可被公众与监管方审计。

Method: 系统由三部分组成。输入端：选取19个公开基准，按EU GPAI Code of Practice的四类系统风险（CBRN、网络攻击、有害操纵、失控）归类，形成风险—基准映射表。评测模块：对每个模型在基准上施加保害扰动（harm-preserving perturbations），即在不改变原始危害语义的前提下变换题目表述，并叠加模拟部署情境，以生成多情境评测样本；评测结果同时记录原始危害强度与情境标签。聚合与打分模块：对每个模型在各风险类别下的基准得分进行聚合，支持两种聚合策略——平均聚合与最坏情况聚合；同时允许调节模型能力对总分的权重，从而暴露被平均分掩盖的尾部风险。验证模块：使用LLM法官对模型输出自动打分，并以人类评分者作为参照计算一致性（κ=0.78–0.82），同时通过盲审评估扰动变换是否保持原始危害（83%保持）。输出层：交互式仪表盘展示风险评分，用户可切换聚合方式、调整能力权重，并逐层追溯到具体基准证据，形成可审计的证据链。研究比较了18个模型在两种聚合下的分数，发现最坏情况聚合下分数下降14–37分；并通过N=21的问卷评估可理解性与设置切换意愿。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最坏情况聚合下的分数下降", "value": "14-37分（18个模型）", "setting": "Systemic Risk Index，平均聚合 vs 最坏情况聚合对比"}, {"metric": "LLM法官与人类评分者一致性", "value": "κ = 0.78–0.82", "setting": "与人类-人类一致性相比较的评分者间一致性"}, {"metric": "盲审中保持原始危害的变换比例", "value": "83%", "setting": "对采样扰动变换的盲审计"}, {"metric": "用户调查中认为分数易理解且鼓励切换评测设置的比例", "value": "多数参与者（N=21）", "setting": "仪表盘可用性调查"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Systemic Risk Index：把19个公开基准映射到EU GPAI行为准则定义的四类系统风险（CBRN、网络攻击、有害操纵、失控）；引入保害扰动（harm-preserving perturbations）与模拟部署情境进行评测，检验风险表现对情境变化的稳健性；交互式仪表盘支持平均聚合与最坏情况聚合切换、可调模型能力权重，并将每个风险等级追溯到具体基准证据；使用LLM法官自动打分，并验证其与人类评分者的一致性（κ=0.78-0.82）可与人类间一致性相比；通过盲审验证83%的采样变换保持原始危害，为扰动有效性提供质量保证

Abstract: Claims about AI safety reach audiences well beyond the AI community, yet many rely on opaque evidence or static assessments, when supporting evidence is accessible at all. We present the Systemic Risk Index, an open evaluation pipeline and dashboard built to make empirical evidence more transparent and traceable to the public. Our work organizes 19 public benchmarks into four systemic-risk categories defined by the EU GPAI Code of Practice---CBRN, cyber offense, harmful manipulation, and loss of control---and evaluates models using harm-preserving perturbations and simulated deployment contexts. The interactive dashboard lets users alternate between average and worst-case aggregation, vary how model capability affects the aggregate score, and trace each risk rating to its benchmark evidence. Across 18 models, scores fall by 14 to 37 points under worst-case aggregation, highlighting information that can be hidden by an average assessment of model risk. LLM judges show agreement with human graders comparable to human--human agreement ($\kappa = 0.78\text{--}0.82$), and a blind audit finds that $83\%$ of sampled transformations preserve the original harm. In a survey ($N = 21$), most participants report that scores are easy to understand and that the dashboard encouraged them to view model evaluations under different settings

</details>


### [71] [Learning the Cost of Reliable Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28322)
*Dimitrios Rontogiannis,Ander Artola Velasco,Manuel Gomez Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个LLM服务采购平台，通过逆向第二价格拍卖和在线质量学习，在保证质量阈值下将查询路由至最具成本竞争力的提供商，实验显示定价利润在10%至71%之间，揭示了固定价格市场的低效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基准测试与路由平台上，提供商通常采用固定每token价格，用户无法为任务获得最具竞争力的价格；需要设计一种采购平台，通过提供商竞争为每个任务定价，同时保证质量水平。

Method: 该论文设计了一个采购平台，作为LLM提供商与终端用户之间的中介。平台顺序接收用户查询，并通过逆向第二价格拍卖将查询路由给模型提供商。在拍卖中，提供商提交其服务该查询的平均成本估计作为竞价；平台选择中标者，并向中标者支付第二价格（即次低竞价），以此激励提供商如实报价。同时，平台在线学习每个提供商的服务质量，维护一个质量估计。路由时，平台仅在质量估计达到用户指定质量阈值的提供商中选择成本最具竞争力的一个。通过持续的路由和观察，平台逐步更新对各提供商成本和质量的估计，实现在保证质量前提下的成本最小化。实验使用多个Llama和Qwen系列LLM，在数学推理和问答基准上进行验证，测量最具成本竞争力提供商的定价利润。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 设计了一个由提供商竞争驱动的token价格采购平台；采用逆向第二价格拍卖顺序路由查询，激励提供商如实报告其服务查询的平均成本估计；平台在路由过程中在线学习每个提供商的质量，并在满足质量阈值的提供商中逐步将查询路由到最具成本竞争力的提供商；通过多个LLM系列和基准实验验证了设计，展示了不同任务和质量阈值下最具成本竞争力提供商的定价利润差异显著（10%-71%），表明固定价格市场存在显著低效

Abstract: Benchmarking and routing platforms increasingly act as intermediaries connecting large language model providers with end-users. However, providers on these platforms typically use a fixed price per token, preventing users from achieving the most competitive price for their tasks. % workloads. In this work, we design a procurement platform where token prices for each task are driven by provider competition, enabling users to secure competitive pricing for guaranteed quality levels. To this end, the platform sequentially routes queries via a reverse second-price auction that incentivizes model providers to truthfully bid their best estimate of the average cost to serve a user's query. As it routes queries, the platform learns the quality offered by each provider and progressively routes queries to the most cost-competitive provider among those meeting a desired quality threshold. To validate our design, we conduct experiments with multiple LLMs from the \texttt{Llama} and \texttt{Qwen} families on popular mathematical reasoning and question-answering benchmarks. The results show that the pricing margin of the most cost-competitive provider on our platform varies significantly---from $10\%$ to $71\%$---depending on the task and quality threshold. This suggests a substantial inefficiency in the current fixed-price market, and it demonstrates that our platform may enable users to capture maximum savings whenever competitive market conditions permit.

</details>


### [72] [Learning What to Activate: Combinatorial Capability Allocation for Long-Horizon Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27869)
*Wenhao Yuan,Chenchen Lin,Jian Chen,Jinfeng Xu,Shuo Yang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向长时程多模态智能体提出组合式能力分配框架CoCA，通过on-policy比较学习与双层次蒸馏获得轻量策略，推理时动态选择能力子集，降低开销并适应阶段需求，在长时程环境和能力需求变化实验中优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程多模态智能体在不同交互阶段对感知、检索、推理、验证、执行等能力的需求不同，固定能力集或预定义工作流会导致大量计算开销且无法适应阶段依赖的能力需求，即如何在每个交互阶段选择代价敏感的能力子集。

Method: CoCA整体流程如下：在每个交互阶段，系统需要从一组专门能力（感知、检索、推理、验证、执行等）中选择一个代价敏感的子集。训练时，首先在student策略访问的状态上，由更强的teacher策略基于当前已选子集对候选能力的边际净值进行条件比较，产生稀疏的条件比较信号。然后使用条件效用模型将这些比较信号转化为自回归的能力子集策略，从而避免对所有可能子集的显式枚举。为缓解分布不匹配，引入双层次on-policy蒸馏：一是在环境状态层面，使student在自身访问的状态分布上学习；二是在子集构造过程中，处理部分子集（partial subsets）带来的分布差异。最后，通过轨迹级强化学习对蒸馏后的策略进行精炼，优化目标包括任务成功率、激活代价和分配稳定性。推理时，分配完全由轻量student策略完成，不需要teacher查询或在线更新，从而降低计算开销并实现可部署的能力调度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将长时程智能体能力调度建模为组合式能力分配问题，在阶段层面选择代价敏感的能力子集；提出基于稀疏条件比较的on-policy学习框架CoCA，在student策略访问的状态上由teacher比较候选能力的边际净值；采用条件效用模型将比较转化为自回归能力子集策略，避免显式枚举；提出双层次on-policy蒸馏，缓解环境状态分布和子集构造过程中的部分子集分布不匹配；使用轨迹级强化学习将蒸馏策略进一步优化至任务成功、激活代价和分配稳定性目标；推理时仅使用轻量student策略进行分配，无需teacher查询或在线更新

Abstract: Long-horizon multimodal agents rely on specialized capabilities for perception, retrieval, reasoning, verification, and execution. Existing designs typically activate a fixed capability set or invoke a predefined workflow, incurring substantial computational overhead while failing to accommodate stage-dependent capability demands. In this paper, we study the \textit{combinatorial capability allocation} problem for long-horizon multimodal agent systems, where the system selects a cost-sensitive subset of specialized capabilities at each interaction stage, which is nontrivial since capability values depend on the selected subset, while previous allocations alter the states encountered by subsequent decisions. We introduce \textsc{CoCA}, an on-policy learning framework that recovers a deployable capability-subset policy from sparse conditional comparisons. On states visited by the student policy, the stronger teacher compares the marginal net values of candidate capabilities, conditioned on the currently selected subset. Then, we adopt a conditional utility model to transform such comparisons into an autoregressive capability-subset policy, avoiding explicit enumeration. We further introduce dual-level on-policy distillation to address distribution mismatch both across environment states and within the partial subsets encountered during set construction. Finally, trajectory-level reinforcement learning refines the distilled policy toward task success, activation cost, and allocation stability. At inference time, allocation is performed solely by the lightweight student policy without teacher queries or online updates. Experiments on long-horizon multimodal environments and controlled capability-demand shifts demonstrate the superiority of our method over the state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [73] [Ask Which, Not How Good: Sizing Benchmarks Scored by an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27787)
*Atul Anand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次测量LLM裁判基准的分辨率，发现单裁判点式评分下存在与项目数无关的泛化上限，成对偏好可大幅降低系统×裁判交互方差，并揭露了显著的顺序偏差和普遍的不确定性报告缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判评分的基准测试（judged benchmarks）的分辨率从未被测量；在单裁判的点式评分下，即使增加测试项，系统间差异的可泛化性也存在理论上限，导致许多已发表的改进声明不可靠。

Method: 论文并非提出新的模型架构，而是建立一套基于泛化理论的测量框架，用于量化LLM裁判基准的分辨率。输入是373,019条LLM裁判判断，每条判断关联一个系统（被评估模型）、一个测试项目和一个裁判。通过方差分解将总方差拆分为系统主效应（σ²_s）、项目主效应、裁判主效应、系统×项目交互、系统×裁判交互（σ²_sj）、项目×裁判交互及残差。核心发现是单裁判点式评分下，泛化系数（generalizability coefficient）随项目数增加渐近于σ²_s/(σ²_s+σ²_sj)，因为σ²_sj不随项目数n_i缩放。项目饱和但裁判不饱和，因此目标接近该上限时，所需项目数发散。进一步将同一评估以成对偏好形式运行并交换呈现顺序，σ²_sj降低两个数量级，泛化系数升至0.986，单裁判即可。此外，论文统计了成对呈现的顺序偏差，并对628篇arXiv论文进行审计，用两个独立模型编码评估实践，与盲人编码对比验证（κ=0.73）。训练目标、推理路径等不适用于本文；实验设计基于已有LLM裁判输出，无新模型训练或部署。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "单裁判点式评分泛化系数上限", "value": "σ²_s/(σ²_s+σ²_sj)", "setting": "泛化理论分解373,019条判断"}, {"metric": "成对偏好泛化系数上限", "value": "0.986", "setting": "bootstrap [0.934,1.000]，11个系统"}, {"metric": "位置偏差", "value": "8.6个百分点", "setting": "成对呈现中先呈现系统胜率优势"}, {"metric": "实测地板范围", "value": "0.41-1.24分", "setting": "0-5分制，原生项目数"}, {"metric": "MT-Bench改进低于地板比例", "value": "17/17", "setting": "与MT-Bench自身地板精确匹配比较"}, {"metric": "胜率声明低于成对地板比例", "value": "70%", "setting": "53个已发表胜率比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将系统作为测量对象，使用泛化理论（generalizability theory）将373,019条LLM裁判判断分解为系统、项目、裁判及交互分量。；揭示单裁判点式评分下的结构性上限：泛化系数渐近于σ²_s/(σ²_s+σ²_sj)，不随项目数增加而提升，因系统×裁判交互项不依赖项目数。；证明成对偏好（pairwise preference）在两个呈现顺序下均可将σ²_sj降低两个数量级，使单裁判足够，上限升至0.986（bootstrap [0.934,1.000]）。；发现成对呈现中位置偏差：先呈现的系统胜率高出8.6个百分点，是所恢复的53个已发表胜率比较中位数改进的1.23倍。；测量出基准测试的实测地板效应：原生项目数下0-5分制的地板为0.41-1.24分，对比中位数报告的改进仅0.28分；所有17个MT-Bench改进低于其自身地板，70%的胜率声明低于成对地板。；对628篇arXiv论文进行审计（双模型独立编码，与盲人编码一致性κ=0.73），发现不足四分之一论文说明评估是否重复运行，仅46-67%报告任何形式的不确定性。

Abstract: Benchmarks scored by an LLM judge routinely adjudicate differences of a tenth of a point, but the resolution of those benchmarks has never been measured. Existing sample-complexity work covers accuracy benchmarks and leaves the judged case open. Treating the system as the object of measurement, we decompose 373,019 judgments into system, item, judge and interaction components using generalizability theory. The central result is structural: under a single judge, generalizability asymptotes to sigma2_s/(sigma2_s+sigma2_sj) regardless of item count, because the system-by-judge term carries no n_i. Items saturate; judges do not. The item cost of a target diverges as the target nears that ceiling. The ceiling is a property of pointwise rubric scoring, not of LLM judging. Run as a pairwise preference in both presentation orders, sigma2_sj falls two orders of magnitude below sigma2_s and the ceiling rises to 0.986 (bootstrap [0.934, 1.000] on 11 systems), so one judge suffices. Pairwise buys a different problem: a system presented first wins 8.6 percentage points more often than the same system presented second, a bias 1.23x the median improvement claimed in the 53 published win-rate comparisons we recovered. Protocol design dominates panel size. Measured floors are 0.41-1.24 points on a 0-5 scale at native item counts, against a median reported improvement of 0.28 points; on the one benchmark recurring often enough for an exactly matched comparison, all 17 recovered MT-Bench improvements fall below MT-Bench's own floor, and 70% of the win-rate claims fall below the pairwise floor. An audit of 628 arXiv papers, double-coded by two independent models and validated against blind human coding (kappa=0.73), finds fewer than one paper in four states whether its evaluation was run more than once, and only 46-67% report uncertainty of any kind.

</details>


### [74] [Alignment of LRMs via Counter-Aligned Few-Shot Conversation Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27763)
*Xiangyu Zhou,Saleh Zare Zade,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了大推理模型（LRM）通过对话接口被反对齐少样本对话操控的风险（SRCF攻击），并提出了后训练防御方法ARCF。ARCF通过让模型在反对齐上下文下仍坚持对齐目标，在不降低模型效用的前提下提升安全性和有用性，兼容已有后训练方法。该工作属于LLM后训练对齐与推理模型安全方向，对构建可靠LLM系统有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）在显式思维链（CoT）推理和长上下文窗口下，可通过对话接口被注入反对齐少样本示例，导致对有害查询生成不安全回答、对良性查询产生无端拒答；同时需要一种后训练防御方法在不降低模型效用的前提下提升安全性和有用性。

Method: 论文未提供完整的模型架构细节，但可推断其工作流程如下：
- 输入表示：大推理模型（LRM）接收多轮对话历史，包含前置的少样本对话示例（每个示例带有显式思维链痕迹）和当前用户查询。
- 攻击流程（SRCF）：在目标查询前拼接精心构造的反对齐少样本对话，这些对话中助手对有害问题的回答是安全的、对良性问题的回答是拒答的，通过上下文学习使模型在推理过程中产生表征漂移，导致对后续有害查询生成不安全内容，或对良性查询无端拒绝。攻击仅通过对话接口进行，不需要模型参数或梯度。
- 防御流程（ARCF）：在模型后训练阶段，将模型暴露于反对齐的对话上下文（即包含反例的少样本对话），同时强制模型输出对齐的目标（即对有害查询安全拒绝，对良性查询积极回答）。通过这种方式让模型学习在存在反例上下文时仍保持对齐，从而抵御SRCF攻击。ARCF兼容现有后训练方法（如RLHF、DPO、SFT等），可叠加使用。
- 目标函数：未明确披露，但ARCF在暴露于反对齐对话时对齐目标通过微调损失（如SFT损失或偏好优化损失）来实现。
- 推理/服务流程：部署时，模型在推理阶段接收多轮对话输入，若攻击者注入反对齐少样本对话，ARCF防御应使模型保持安全与有用。论文未披露具体的推理硬件、延迟或吞吐资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化SRCF攻击：仅通过对话接口，在输入前拼接包含显式CoT痕迹的反对齐少样本对话，无需访问模型参数或梯度，即可系统性地操控LRM的推理过程。；揭示攻击机制：发现对抗泛化问题导致表征漂移，使良性和有害输入的表征向相似方向偏移。；提出ARCF防御：在上下文暴露于反对齐对话的同时强制执行对齐目标，且兼容现有后训练方法，持续提升安全性和有用性而不损害效用。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) rely on explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and large context windows to achieve strong performance on complex tasks, but these features also introduce new attack surfaces. We show that LRMs' reasoning processes can be systematically steered by prepending counter-aligned few-shot conversations containing explicit CoT traces, leading to unsafe generations on harmful queries and unwarranted refusals on benign ones. We formalize this attack as SRCF (Steering Reasoning via Counter-Aligned Few-shot Conversations) that operates solely through a flexible conversational interface and requires no access to the model's parameters and gradients. Our key insight is that SRCF exploits an adversarial generalization issue that induces a representation drift, causing the representations of benign and harmful inputs to shift in a similar direction. This observation motivates our post-training defense, ARCF (Aligning Reasoning via Counter-Aligned Few-Shot Conversations), which exposes models to counter-aligned conversational contexts while enforcing aligned targets. ARCF is compatible with existing post-training methods and consistently improves safety and helpfulness without degrading utility.

</details>


### [75] [WhatWorkedBench: Benchmarking Experimental Understanding in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27490)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WhatWorkedBench是一个评估AI智能体实验理解能力的基准，要求智能体在有限实验预算下预测组件变更的效果。该基准包含36个任务、30个数据源、8种工作流类型和1248条配置记录。实验表明，高斯过程拟合智能体观测比pair-effect ridge在效果恢复和配置优化上更优，且编码代码等价性可显著提升GP性能。该工作为研究实验智能体、自适应实验设计和数值推理提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何量化和评估AI研究智能体在有限实验预算下对组件变更如何影响结果的预测能力，即实验理解（experimental understanding）。

Method: WhatWorkedBench是一个评估框架：智能体首先检查给定任务的代码和数据，然后在预算内选择实验测量（如运行配置获取得分），基于这些观测，智能体需提交一个响应面，即对所有组件设置组合的得分预测表。参考效应通过穷举CPU执行获得，用于计算每个组件在固定其他组件时的变化效应。评估指标包括数值控制记录（4206条）和智能体episodes（108个）。论文还比较了pair-effect ridge和高斯过程（GP）两种拟合方法：pair-effect ridge从8个新测量中推断组件效应并选择最优配置；GP则对智能体观测进行拟合以提升效果恢复。此外，通过编码代码等价性（行为相同的配置）可进一步提升GP恢复率。整个流程无需训练大模型，而是聚焦于智能体在实验设计、测量选择和数值推断中的表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "effect recovery", "value": "0.632 -> 0.698", "setting": "原始Flash队列，GP拟合智能体观测"}, {"metric": "effect recovery", "value": "0.621 -> 0.720", "setting": "额外队列，GP拟合智能体观测"}, {"metric": "family-macro recovery", "value": "0.303 -> 0.455", "setting": "6个完成的beat-detection和graph提交，同观测GP"}, {"metric": "GP recovery", "value": "0.248 -> 0.462", "setting": "6个含6个二元选项工作流，20次新测量，编码代码等价性"}, {"metric": "最优选择成功率", "value": "15/22", "setting": "8次新测量，pair-effect ridge选择最优"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出WhatWorkedBench基准，通过穷举CPU执行获得组件变化的参考效应，构建包含36个任务、30个数据源、8种工作流类型、1248条配置记录的预测任务。；智能体需检查代码、选择测量并提交响应面（预测所有组件配置的得分表），以评估其从有限实验中推断配置效果的能力。；使用高斯过程（GP）拟合智能体观测数据，相比pair-effect ridge在效果恢复和配置优化上表现更好，并展示了编码代码等价性可进一步提升GP恢复率。

Abstract: AI research agents need reliable knowledge of how their experiments change outcomes. We introduce WhatWorkedBench to measure experimental understanding, the accuracy of predictions about component changes after budgeted experimentation. Agents inspect code, select measurements, and submit a response surface, a table predicting scores for every configuration of component settings. Exhaustive CPU execution supplies reference effects for changing each component while holding the others fixed. These effects capture combinations of changes across 36 tasks from 30 data sources and 8 workflow types, with 1248 configuration records. Core evaluation combines 4,206 numerical-control records across all eight families and 108 agent episodes across the original six. At eight new measurements, pair-effect ridge selects an optimum on 15 of 22 sources and limits every effect error to 10% of score range on three. Fitting a Gaussian process (GP) to the same agent observations raises effect recovery, accuracy relative to true effect magnitude, from 0.632 to 0.698 in the original Flash cohort and from 0.621 to 0.720 in an additional cohort. On six completed beat-detection and graph submissions, the same-observation GP raises family-macro recovery from 0.303 to 0.455. On six workflows with six binary options at 20 new measurements, encoding code equivalences, configurations with identical behavior, raises GP recovery from 0.248 to 0.462. WhatWorkedBench supports research on experimental agents, adaptive experimental design, numerical inference, and use of program structure.

</details>


### [76] [State-Grounded Conditioning: Wrapping User-Facing LLM Agents Where Direction Depends on Live State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27606)
*Qi Liu,Xiaoyang Yuan,Yubin Ruan,Zhuomeng Zhang,Wenjin Wang,Di Wu,Mingye Xu,Xinyi Mou,Xingxi Yin,Ke Feng,Zixun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 State-Grounded Conditioning 设计原则，将实时状态依赖的控制外置为规则内核，并用 Perception/Grounding/Interaction 三层包装器缓解用户侧 LLM Agent 的“方向漂移”。在游戏教练场景的 200 会话 benchmark 上，轮级接地准确率从 61.1%/69.8% 提升到 96.7%，会话级从 20.0%/26.5% 提升到 83.5%，接地失败事件下降约 78%，首 token 延迟保持 1.5s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户侧 LLM Agent 依赖实时状态进行决策时会出现“方向漂移”：任务已完成但所选方向与当前状态不符，且直接依赖 LLM 内部状态推理会导致延迟高、事实接地差。

Method: SGC 将依赖状态的用户侧 Agent 控制从 LLM 内部推理外置为规则内核，作用于结构化输入与三个状态切片（游戏状态、会话历史、实时库存等）。系统由 Perception、Grounding、Interaction 三层包装器组成，包装器之间声明显式的条件化依赖顺序：Perception 负责将结构化状态输入转换为模型可用的条件化表示并控制首 token 延迟；Grounding 负责将生成内容锚定到当前状态切片以保证事实接地；Interaction 负责将接地后的输出组织为符合教练式引导推进的对话交互。三层包装器按累积方式组合，消融实验显示各层带来互补的增量准确率提升。训练目标与损失函数未披露；推理流程中，规则内核先于或环绕 LLM 生成执行，从而避免方向漂移。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "turn-level grounded accuracy", "value": "96.7% (vs 61.1% Prompting, 69.8% PE-Agent)", "setting": "200 会话匿名 benchmark，约 1000 轮 assistant 输出，人工标注"}, {"metric": "session-level grounded accuracy", "value": "83.5% (vs 20.0% Prompting, 26.5% PE-Agent)", "setting": "同一 benchmark 的会话级聚合"}, {"metric": "session-level grounding-failure incidents", "value": "相对最强 baseline 下降约 78%", "setting": "同一 benchmark"}, {"metric": "mean first-token latency", "value": "1.5s (Perception wrapper)，对比 PE-Agent 在生产工具调用 harness 中 6.1s", "setting": "首 token 延迟测量"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 State-Grounded Conditioning (SGC) 设计原则，将依赖状态的控制外置到结构化输入上的规则内核；定义并形式化 direction drift 这一失败类别；设计 Perception、Grounding、Interaction 三层包装器，并显式声明条件化依赖；通过累积消融展示三层包装器的互补增量收益，并提出状态切片近似正交性假设

Abstract: We introduce State-Grounded Conditioning (SGC), a design principle for user-facing LLM agents that must condition on live user state (game state, session history, live inventory), and a distinct failure class we call direction drift: task-complete responses whose chosen direction misaligns with the current state. SGC externalises state-dependent control into rule kernels over structured inputs and three primary state slices, via Perception, Grounding, and Interaction wrappers with explicit conditioning dependencies. We evaluate SGC on a 200-session anonymised benchmark ($\approx$1,000 assistant model turns) from an in-game conversational coaching agent that guides players through consecutive competitive matches, reporting mean first-token latency and five human-annotated dialogue-quality metrics that jointly cover factual grounding and coach-like guidance progression. The Perception wrapper holds mean first-token latency at 1.5s (vs. 6.1s for PE-Agent inside a production tool-use harness); enabling all three wrappers lifts turn-level grounded accuracy from 61.1%/69.8% (Prompting / PE-Agent) to 96.7% and session-level grounded accuracy from 20.0%/26.5% to 83.5%; session-level grounding-failure incidents drop by $\approx$78% relative to the strongest baseline. A cumulative ablation shows complementary incremental gains as the wrappers are added. These results inform approximate state-slice orthogonality, without establishing independent per-wrapper effects.

</details>


### [77] [Just-in-Time Memory: Learning to Curate Task-Adaptive Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27334)
*Yefan Zhou,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JitMem，把Agent记忆策展从写时改到读时：保留原始轨迹，在已知当前任务时由curator合成任务自适应记忆payload，并可用当前任务成功直接训练，绕开长时程信用分配。在ALFWorld、WebShop、τ²-bench上分别比最强基线高出16.2、16.3、3.9个绝对成功率点，未训练curator已具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic记忆系统在write-time固定策展记忆（反思、工作流、技能、推理策略），必须在未知未来query前决定存储内容，不可逆丢弃信息，且生成query无关的摘要；同时write-time策展器的价值需等到相关query到达才能体现，形成长时程信用分配难题。

Method: 系统保留任务执行产生的原始trajectory作为记忆库，不做write-time压缩。给定新任务时，先按相似度检索相关trajectory traces，随后由memory curator接收检索到的traces与当前任务，生成紧凑的、面向当前任务的自适应payload（而非query无关的固定摘要）。该payload立即被当前任务的Agent消费，因此策展过程可端到端地以当前任务是否成功为奖励进行训练，无需等待未来query到来后的延迟效用信号，也无需人为把相关任务分组。推理路径为：任务输入→检索历史原始trajectory→curator合成任务自适应记忆payload→Agent基于该payload与任务执行。对比方法包括no-memory agent、启发式write-time记忆方法以及可学习的write-time记忆方法。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "绝对成功率提升", "value": "+16.2", "setting": "ALFWorld，相对最强基线"}, {"metric": "绝对成功率提升", "value": "+16.3", "setting": "WebShop，相对最强基线"}, {"metric": "绝对成功率提升", "value": "+3.9", "setting": "τ²-bench，相对最强基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将记忆策展从write-time推迟到read-time：保留原始trajectory，在已知当前任务时再合成紧凑的任务自适应payload；策展器可在同一任务上被直接消费，从而用即时任务成功作为监督信号训练，避免延迟效用与人工任务分组的需要；实验显示即使未训练的策展器也已达到或超过write-time基线，说明read-time任务自适应策展本身是主要收益来源，训练进一步叠加提升

Abstract: Agentic memory systems reuse past experience to improve future performance, yet most existing designs curate memory at write time: once a task is completed, its trajectory is distilled into a fixed artifact, such as a reflection, workflow, skill, or reasoning strategy, that is later retrieved by similarity. This forces the system to decide what is worth remembering before the future query is known, irreversibly discarding information and producing a query-independent summary that must serve many possible downstream tasks. Learning such a write-time curator is also difficult because the value of a storage decision may only become apparent when a relevant query arrives, potentially many tasks later, creating a long-horizon credit-assignment problem. We instead retain raw trajectories and defer curation until read time, when the current task is known. Given the retrieved traces and the new task, a memory curator synthesizes a compact, task-adaptive payload tailored to the immediate need. Because this payload is consumed on the same task, the curator can be trained directly from immediate task success, avoiding delayed utility signals and the need to artificially group related tasks. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and $\tau^2$-bench, our Just-in-Time Memory (JitMem) consistently outperforms no-memory agents as well as heuristic and learned write-time memory methods, improving over the strongest baseline by 16.2, 16.3, and 3.9 absolute success-rate points, respectively. Notably, even an untrained curator is already competitive with or surpasses these baselines, showing that task-adaptive read-time curation itself is a major source of the gain; training the curator further compounds the improvement.

</details>


### [78] [Verifiable Hidden Dynamics Play: Generating Agentic RL Environments from Solved Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27321)
*Xinjie Shen,Wei Fan,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Yang Su,Chuqiao Kuang,Yinger Zhang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VHD-Play先求解数学模型，再将决策过程渲染为有状态工具，使可执行动力学与轨迹评分参考同源，低成本生成3300个agentic RL环境；训练Qwen3.6-35B-A3B使五家族诊断平均分从0.204升至0.815，并泛化到未见机制家族及函数调用、旅行规划、365天电商等外部基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体训练面临长时程、状态演化、决策相互依赖和延迟结果，扩展训练需要多样agentic环境、可靠结果信号和低扩展成本；现有环境生成流程先构造环境再定义结果规则或标注轨迹，导致动力学与评测需要事后对齐。

Method: VHD-Play是一个反向依赖的环境生成与训练流水线。输入不是预先写好的任务或轨迹，而是先采样一个数学模型并对其进行求解，得到可执行的隐藏动力学和该模型的轨迹评分参考。随后，一个基于语料的setter将该已求解模型的决策过程渲染为有状态工具：这些工具承载环境状态和转移逻辑，使智能体通过多步工具调用与状态交互，而不是直接面对一次性写出的问题。由于可执行动力学和轨迹评分参考都继承自同一个已求解模型，环境的结果规则与轨迹评估天然对齐，无需事后标注或对齐。生成的3,300个环境覆盖多样化机制家族。训练时，Qwen3.6-35B-A3B在这些环境上使用继承自模型的轨迹评分进行agentic强化学习/后训练；评分为基于可执行状态的参考信号。推理时，模型作为agent在状态化工具环境中逐步交互，通过函数调用推进状态，最终由同一求解模型对应的评分参考判定轨迹结果。论文还比较了隐藏参数与揭示参数的状态化版本，表明大部分可学习差距来自有状态交互而非底层问题求解；冻结35B setter可渲染更大环境，规模匹配训练使增益随机制规模和时程增长而保持。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "五家族诊断中的平均agentic分数", "value": "0.204 → 0.815", "setting": "在三个机制家族上训练Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，并在五家族诊断上评测"}, {"metric": "E-Commerce Bench", "value": "训练checkpoint在每次运行中完成且未破产，并超过Qwen3.7-Max", "setting": "365天电商外部基准"}, {"metric": "外部基准迁移", "value": "在通用函数调用、旅行规划、365天电商上出现增益", "setting": "使用生成基底训练后的模型评测外部基准"}, {"metric": "留出实例和未见机制家族", "value": "三个训练家族的留出实例以及八个未见机制家族上均出现增益", "setting": "机制家族泛化评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 反转依赖顺序：先采样并求解数学模型，再让基于语料的setter将其决策过程渲染为有状态工具，使可执行动力学和轨迹评分参考继承自同一已求解模型。；以低成本生成3300个多样化agentic环境，每个成本仅为几美分。；通过状态化工具隐藏/揭示参数，将可学习差距主要定位在有状态交互而非底层问题求解。；训练Qwen3.6-35B-A3B在三个机制家族上，平均agentic分数从0.204提升到0.815，并迁移到留出实例和八个未见机制家族。；冻结35B setter支持更大环境，规模匹配训练在机制规模和时程增长时仍保持增益，形成可进化的训练基底。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly face long-horizon tasks with evolving state, interdependent decisions, and delayed outcomes. Scaling their training requires diverse agentic environments, dependable outcome signals, and low extension cost. Existing generation pipelines commonly construct an environment before defining its outcome rule or annotating its trajectories, leaving dynamics and evaluation to be aligned post hoc. VHD-Play reverses this dependency by sampling and solving a mathematical model before a corpus-grounded setter renders its decision process as stateful tools. The executable dynamics and trajectory-scoring reference are inherited from the same solved model. The pipeline produces 3,300 diverse agentic environments at a cost of a few cents each. Training Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on three families raises its mean agentic score from 0.204 to 0.815 in a five-family diagnostic. Gains also appear on held-out instances from all three training families and eight unseen mechanism families, then extend beyond the generated substrate to external benchmarks for general function calling, travel planning, and 365-day e-commerce. On E-Commerce Bench, the trained checkpoint completes every run without bankruptcy and exceeds Qwen3.7-Max. We compare written-out problems with stateful versions that reveal or hide their parameters. The comparison shows that most of the learnable gap lies in stateful interaction rather than underlying problem solving. A frozen 35B setter realizes larger environments, and scale-matched training retains gains as mechanism size and horizon grow, indicating the potential for an evolving training substrate.

</details>


### [79] [Stable Geometry with Divergent Task Evidence for Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27332)
*Mingxuan Wang,Fei Luo,Bo Wang,Guorun Yao,Yinglong Guo,Chao Ning,Hongyue Chen,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现长程 Agent 历史虽具低维几何结构，但几何冗余不足以判断安全压缩；提出免训练压缩器 GEM，优先保护任务与执行证据、再以几何残差补全覆盖，将平均 token 用量从 2.69M 降至 2.11M（降低 21.4%）并保持任务奖励相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 Agent 交互历史不断累积导致上下文与推理成本增长；仅依据几何冗余进行压缩并不安全，因为全局几何相似的结构可能保留截然不同的任务证据。

Method: GEM 是一个免训练（training-free）的 Agent 历史压缩器，输入为长程 Agent 逐渐累积的交互历史（上下文块/block 序列）。其端到端流程为：首先对交互历史进行证据识别，优先保护与任务目标及执行动作相关的任务证据（task evidence 与 execution evidence）；在保留这些关键证据后，再利用几何残差（geometric residuals）对剩余覆盖进行补全，从而在减少 token 数量的同时尽量不损害后续决策所需信息。论文强调压缩选择过程不依赖额外训练，而是直接作用于历史表示与选择策略。其核心动机来自实验观察：Agent 历史虽呈现强低维几何结构，但全局几何相似度（如 centroid 相似度）无法反映实际保留的任务证据量；在相同保留 block 数下，仅按几何选择与按证据感知选择的 next action Top-3 保留率分别为 0.31 与 0.69，而 centroid 相似度均为 0.98，说明必须显式优化任务证据保留而非几何覆盖。受控替换实验进一步显示，动作相关信息可被大幅改动而全局几何度量几乎不变。推理/服务路径为：在 Agent 每步决策前，GEM 对累积历史执行免训练压缩，输出更短但保留关键任务与执行证据的上下文，再交由下游 Agent/LLM 进行下一步动作预测，从而降低上下文长度与推理成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "next action Top-3 保留率", "value": "从 0.31 提升到 0.69", "setting": "相同保留 block 数量下，证据感知选择 vs. 仅几何选择，centroid 相似度保持 0.98"}, {"metric": "平均总 token 用量", "value": "从 2.69M 降至 2.11M，降低 21.4%", "setting": "长程 Agent 任务，GEM 压缩后任务奖励相当"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现并量化几何冗余与任务证据之间的错位：在相同保留 block 数量下，证据感知选择将 next action Top-3 保留率从 0.31 提升到 0.69，而 centroid 相似度仍保持 0.98。；通过受控替换实验证明，动作相关信息可被大幅改变而全局几何度量几乎不变，说明几何度量不足以作为安全压缩的判据。；提出无需训练的压缩器 GEM（Geometry Guided Evidence Preserving Memory），先保护任务与执行证据，再用几何残差完成覆盖。；在长程 Agent 任务上实现平均总 token 用量从 2.69M 降至 2.11M（21.4% 降低），同时保持任务奖励相当。

Abstract: Long horizon agents accumulate growing interaction histories that increase context and inference costs. We find that geometric redundancy alone is an insufficient criterion for safe compression. Although agent histories exhibit strong low dimensional structure, similar global geometry can preserve very different amounts of task evidence. At identical retained block counts, evidence aware selection raises next action Top 3 retention from 0.31 to 0.69, while centroid similarity remains 0.98. Controlled replacement further shows that action related information can be substantially altered while global geometric measures remain nearly unchanged. Motivated by this gap between geometry and evidence, we introduce Geometry Guided Evidence Preserving Memory (GEM), a training free compressor that protects task and execution evidence before using geometric residuals to complete coverage. GEM reduces mean combined token usage from 2.69M to 2.11M per task, a 21.4% reduction, while maintaining comparable task reward. Our results show that efficient agent history compression should optimize for preserved task evidence rather than geometric coverage alone.

</details>


### [80] [Hunyuan-A13B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27284)
*Tencent Hunyuan Team,Ao Liu,Botong Zhou,Can Xu,Chayse Zhou,ChenChen Zhang,Chengcheng Xu,Chenhao Wang,Decheng Wu,Dengpeng Wu,Dian Jiao,Dong Du,Dong Wang,Feng Zhang,Fengzong Lian,Guanghui Xu,Guanwei Zhang,Hai Wang,Haipeng Luo,Han Hu,Huilin Xu,Jiajia Wu,Jianchen Zhu,Jianfeng Yan,Jiaqi Zhu,Jihong Zhang,Jinbao Xue,Jun Xia,Junqiang Zheng,Kai Liu,Kai Zhang,Kai Zheng,Kejiao Li,Keyao Wang,Lan Jiang,Lixin Liu,Lulu Wu,Mengyuan Huang,Peijie Yu,Peiqi Wang,Qian Wang,Qianbiao Xiang,Qibin Liu,Qingfeng Sun,Richard Guo,Ruobing Xie,Saiyong Yang,Shaohua Chen,Shihui Hu,Shuai Li,Shuaipeng Li,Shuang Chen,Suncong Zheng,Tao Yang,Tian Zhang,Tinghao Yu,Weidong Han,Weijie Liu,Weijin Zhou,Weikang Wang,Wesleye Chen,Xiao Feng,Xiaoqin Ren,Xingwu Sun,Xiong Kuang,Xuemeng Huang,Xun Cao,Yanfeng Chen,Yang Du,Zhen Yang,Yangyu Tao,Yaping Deng,Yi Shen,Yigeng Hong,Yiqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 腾讯混元团队发布的开源MoE大模型Hunyuan-A13B，总参80B、激活13B，在20T token语料上预训练，经SFT和RL优化，引入双模式思维链自适应推理深度，具有高推理吞吐，适合延迟敏感部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持大模型能力的同时，降低推理计算成本与部署开销，并适应不同复杂度的任务推理深度。

Method: Hunyuan-A13B采用Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构，总参数量80B，但每次推理仅激活13B参数，以平衡模型能力、计算效率和部署成本。模型首先在严格过滤的20T token语料上进行预训练，并特别增强了STEM数据的整理，以提高事实可靠性和推理能力。随后通过高质量的监督微调（SFT）和大规模强化学习（RL）进一步提升性能。模型引入双模式思维链（Chain-of-Thought）框架：对于常规查询采用快速思考模式以减少计算，对于复杂多步问题采用慢速思考模式以增加推理深度。该框架使模型能够根据任务复杂度自适应地调整推理路径。高推理吞吐量设计使其适用于延迟敏感的应用场景。整个系统在推理流程中动态选择激活的专家模块，仅计算13B参数，从而降低计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于Mixture-of-Experts架构的80B总参数、13B激活参数的大语言模型，平衡能力与效率。；使用经过严格过滤的20T token语料预训练，并增强STEM数据整理，提升事实可靠性与推理能力。；高质量监督微调和大规模强化学习进一步提升整体性能。；双模式思维链框架，根据任务复杂度自适应调整推理深度：快速思考处理常规查询，慢速思考处理复杂多步问题。；高推理吞吐量，适合延迟敏感应用。

Abstract: We present Hunyuan-A13B, an open-source large language model based on a Mixture-of-Experts architecture. It contains 80 billion total parameters but activates only 13 billion during inference, balancing model capability, computational efficiency, and deployment cost. The model is pretrained on a rigorously filtered 20T-token corpus with enhanced STEM data curation, improving factual reliability and reasoning ability. High-quality supervised fine-tuning and large-scale reinforcement learning further enhance its overall performance. Hunyuan-A13B also introduces a dual-mode Chain-of-Thought framework that adapts reasoning depth to task complexity: fast thinking for routine queries and slow thinking for complex, multi-step problems. Evaluations show competitive performance across mathematics, science, programming, general language understanding, and agent tasks, often approaching that of much larger models. Its high inference throughput makes it suitable for latency-sensitive applications. We release Hunyuan-A13B to support open research and practical LLM deployment.

</details>


### [81] [EnSIMem: Entity-Structured Indexing for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27279)
*Xuanyu Meng,Xing Fan,Xinyi Fan,Chenlei Guo,Yixuan Xie,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnSIMem 提出实体结构化长期智能体记忆：离线将交互组织为主题 episode 并构建 [entity][entity type][property:value] 对话 grounded 索引，保留源轮次与时间；在线分解证据需求、对齐属性并自适应检索，基于源证据生成回答。在长期智能体记忆基准上取得高准确率，并保持紧凑上下文和良好在线效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统把交互压缩成泛化摘要或检索匿名文本块，导致智能体难以识别正确实体、属性并保留可溯源证据，从而在点式、时序、组合和聚合推理上出错。

Method: EnSIMem 分为离线记忆构建与在线交互两阶段。离线阶段：将持续增长的交互历史按主题组织成相干 episode；对每段 episode 构建对话 grounded 的索引条目，形式为 [entity][entity type][property:value]，并保留对应源对话轮次、时间信息以及可用的多模态字段。在线阶段：将智能体收到的请求分解为多个证据需求；把每个需求的属性与记忆索引进行对齐，通过实体-属性查找和自适应检索收集回答所需的证据；最后智能体基于保留的源证据而非压缩摘要生成响应。该架构解决的核心问题是长交互历史中实体、属性与证据难以精确匹配，并通过 episode 级溯源提升点式、时序、组合与聚合推理的可靠性。摘要未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、硬件、延迟或吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 离线将交互组织为主题的相干 episode，并构建对话 grounded 的 [entity][entity type][property:value] 索引条目。；索引条目保留源对话轮次、时间信息与可用的多模态字段，实现 episode 级证据溯源。；在线将请求分解为证据需求，并把需求属性与记忆索引对齐，再进行实体-属性查询与自适应检索。；智能体基于保留的源证据而非有损摘要生成回答，支撑点式、时序、组合与聚合推理。

Abstract: An agent that interacts with users over long periods must recall facts, preferences, events, and changes from a continuously growing interaction history. Existing memory systems often compress interactions into generic summaries or retrieve anonymous text chunks, making it difficult for an agent to identify the correct entity, property, and supporting evidence. We present EnSIMem, an entity-structured long-term memory architecture for an agent. During offline construction, the system organizes interactions into theme-coherent episodes and builds dialogue-grounded index entries of the form [entity][entity type][property:value]. Each entry preserves its source turns, temporal information, and available multimodal fields. During online interaction, the agent's request is decomposed into evidence requirements whose properties are aligned with the memory index. Entity-property lookup and adaptive retrieval then collect the evidence needed for point, temporal, compositional, and aggregation reasoning. The agent generates its response from the preserved source evidence rather than from lossy memory summaries. On long-term agent-memory benchmarks, EnSIMem achieves high answer accuracy while maintaining compact contexts and favorable online efficiency. These results show that entity-structured indexing and episode-level provenance provide a reliable foundation for long-term memory in agents. The code of our model is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [82] [Policy-as-Skill: Governed LLM Decision Support with Evidence, Deterministic Control, and Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27087)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Policy-as-Skill (PaS)，一个用于 LLM 决策支持的治理运行时，提供证据验证、审查路由、版本控制和审计功能。在 600 个开发任务上，PaS+Audit 在治理和审查指标上优于 LLM+RAG，确定性控制可选择性提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在 LLM 决策支持中实现证据验证、审查路由、版本控制和可审计性，同时平衡准确性与规则干预。

Method: PaS 是一个模块化运行时，将策略功能打包为可执行、带版本的策略能力。论文评估了 13 种方法，固定使用 Gemma4 后端，在 600 个开发任务上测试。PaS+Audit 集成了证据验证、审查路由、版本控制和审计功能，在治理和审查指标上优于 LLM+RAG。确定性控制模块可选择性地应用规则干预，提高聚合准确率，但效果任务依赖性强。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "exact accuracy", "value": "53.8%", "setting": "PaS+Audit，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端"}, {"metric": "macro-F1", "value": "0.346", "setting": "PaS+Audit，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端"}, {"metric": "review F1", "value": "0.854", "setting": "PaS+Audit，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端"}, {"metric": "citation precision", "value": "1.000", "setting": "PaS+Audit，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端"}, {"metric": "policy-reference recall", "value": "0.984", "setting": "PaS+Audit，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端"}, {"metric": "audit completeness", "value": "1.000", "setting": "PaS+Audit，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端"}, {"metric": "aggregate accuracy", "value": "61.2%", "setting": "确定性控制，600 开发任务，固定 Gemma4 后端，任务依赖性强"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Policy-as-Skill (PaS) 模块化运行时，将策略功能封装为可执行、带版本的策略能力；PaS+Audit 在治理和审查指标上优于 LLM+RAG；确定性控制可提高聚合准确率，但任务依赖性强，支持选择性而非普遍性规则干预

Abstract: Organizations increasingly use LLMs for policy, compliance, risk, and operational decision support, requiring evidence validation, review routing, version control, and auditability. We introduce Policy-as-Skill (PaS), a modular runtime that packages these functions as executable, versioned policy capabilities. Thirteen methods are evaluated with a fixed Gemma4 backend on 600 development tasks. PaS+Audit achieves 53.8% exact accuracy, macro-F1 0.346, review F1 0.854, citation precision 1.000, policy-reference recall 0.984, and audit completeness 1.000, outperforming LLM+RAG on most governance and review metrics. Deterministic control raises aggregate accuracy to 61.2% but is strongly task dependent, supporting selective rather than universal rule-based intervention.

</details>


### [83] [Reinforcement Learning with Decomposed Subtasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27035)
*Mattie Terzolo,Mikolaj Sacha,Ayan Sinha,Andrew Rabinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RLDS 及其核心 SDAE，将多轮 LLM 智能体轨迹奖励按固定子任务分类体系拆分，逐子任务计算组相对优势并按重要性加权、把逐 token 信用集中到关键 reflection 步骤附近，替代 GRPO 的整条轨迹标量优势。在 FrozenLake、HotpotQA、ScienceWorld、DeepResearch 四个 agentic 基准上评测：子任务异质性高的 ScienceWorld（+11.5 分）与 FrozenLake（+9.8 分）提升显著，而在 HotpotQA 与 DeepResearch 上差异在噪声范围内，与训练中输出的异质性诊断一致；ScienceWorld 上每步墙钟时间相对标量 GRPO 降低 10.9%。未披露机构、模型规模与训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 等策略梯度方法将在多轮 rollout 中得到的整条轨迹奖励压缩成单一标量后再进入策略更新；当任务由可区分的子技能组合而成、且环境反馈稀疏且延迟时，这种压缩是有损的，优化器必须隐式推断是哪项能力驱动了结果以及应如何改变行为。

Method: RLDS 作用于语言模型智能体的多轮 rollout 训练流程。输入是智能体与环境交互产生的多轮轨迹，轨迹中包含各步骤的动作、观察，以及标记某个子任务执行为关键节点的 reflection。与 GRPO 将整条轨迹奖励压缩为单一标量不同，RLDS 首先依据一个固定分类体系把轨迹级奖励拆分为各子任务的份额；随后在每个子任务维度上按 GRPO 式组相对方式计算该子任务的组相对优势；接着按每个子任务的重要性对该子任务优势加权，并将逐 token 的信用分配集中到标记该子任务执行具有关键意义的 reflection 步骤附近。最终由这些加权后的逐子任务优势共同构成替代标量 GRPO 优势的 SDAE 估计，进入策略更新。训练过程中额外输出异质性诊断，用来指示子任务分解在何处可带来收益。整体目标并非替换策略梯度框架，而是在优势估计环节保留子任务结构，从而避免标量压缩造成的有损信用分配。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "得分提升", "value": "+11.5 points，配对 bootstrap 95% CI [+9.8, +13.3]", "setting": "ScienceWorld，长时程具身科学任务，RLDS 对比标量 GRPO"}, {"metric": "得分提升", "value": "+9.8 points，配对 bootstrap 95% CI [+7.0, +12.8]", "setting": "FrozenLake，稀疏网格导航，RLDS 对比标量 GRPO"}, {"metric": "每步墙钟时间", "value": "-10.9%", "setting": "ScienceWorld，RLDS 对比标量 GRPO，因长 rollout 摊薄固定的 reflect-and-grade 开销"}, {"metric": "得分差异", "value": "在噪声范围内", "setting": "HotpotQA（多跳问答，一个检索工具）与 DeepResearch（长文研究，四个工具，复合 rubric 奖励），诊断预测可恢复空间小"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 RLDS：把轨迹奖励在进入策略更新前按子任务拆分，而非只改进标量奖励设计。；提出 Subtask-Decomposed Advantage Estimation (SDAE)：基于固定分类体系将轨迹奖励拆分为各子任务份额，对每个子任务计算组相对优势。；按子任务重要性对其优势加权，把逐 token 信用集中到标记该子任务执行具有关键意义的 reflection 步骤附近。；训练中输出异质性诊断，用于指示分解何时带来收益，并在 ScienceWorld 上显示相对标量 GRPO 的每步墙钟时间降低。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related policy-gradient methods for training language model agents collapse an entire multi-turn rollout into a single scalar trajectory reward before it enters the policy update. When the task composes distinct skills, especially under sparse and delayed environmental feedback, this collapsing is lossy: the optimizer must implicitly infer which competency drove the outcome and how that should change behavior. We argue the right primitive is not a better scalar but a decomposition: trajectory reward should be split along subtasks before it enters the policy update. We introduce Reinforcement Learning with Decomposed Subtasks (RLDS), whose core is Subtask-Decomposed Advantage Estimation (SDAE): a replacement for the scalar GRPO advantage that splits trajectory reward into per-subtask shares on a fixed taxonomy, computes a group-relative advantage per subtask, and distributes per-token credit by weighting each subtask's advantage by its importance, concentrating it around the step where a reflection marks that subtask's execution as consequential. We evaluate on four agentic benchmarks: FrozenLake (sparse grid navigation), HotpotQA (multi-hop QA, one retrieval tool), ScienceWorld (long-horizon embodied science), and DeepResearch (long-form research, four tools, composite rubric reward). Heterogeneity diagnostics emitted during training show where decomposition pays off - gains scale with subtask heterogeneity, largest on the high-heterogeneity tasks ScienceWorld (+11.5 points, paired-bootstrap 95% CI [+9.8, +13.3]) and FrozenLake (+9.8 points, [+7.0, +12.8]), and within noise on HotpotQA and DeepResearch, where the diagnostics predicted little to recover. ScienceWorld is also more compute-efficient under RLDS than scalar GRPO (-10.9% wall-clock per step), as long rollouts amortize the fixed reflect-and-grade overhead.

</details>


### [84] [TwinCheck: Evidence-Grounded Negative-Twin Verification for Stateful Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26911)
*Jiaxuan Dai,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinCheck提出一种推理时验证策略，通过构造轨迹局部的反事实“负孪生”并与原始动作成对比较，仅在证据条件和结构检查满足且验证器一致偏好孪生时才替换动作。在159个BFCL V4多轮任务上，该方法使GPT-5.6 Sol的任务成功率从45.3%提升至58.5%，且无成功转失败案例，为工具智能体的执行边界修复提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个局部看似合理的工具调用可能使原本成功的智能体轨迹失败；但仅凭怀疑进行干预可能引入新的失败，如何在推理时安全地决定是否替换智能体的候选动作。

Method: TwinCheck是一种推理时验证策略。其输入是智能体的多轮轨迹（包括历史对话、工具调用和解析响应）。核心模块按顺序如下：1）证据条件检查：检查当前轨迹是否满足与轨迹局部失败假设相关的证据条件，仅当满足时才考虑替换提议动作；2）负孪生构造：基于当前轨迹局部地构造一个反事实替代动作（负孪生）；3）结构检查：验证负孪生是否通过预设的结构检查（如格式、可行性等）；4）成对验证：使用成对验证器对原始提议动作和负孪生进行比较，且要求在两种候选顺序（原始第一个或孪生第一个）下验证器都偏好负孪生；5）仅当以上条件全部满足时，替换智能体的提议动作为负孪生。评估采用精确重放配对设置：固定智能体的解析响应和动作，直到第一个被接受的替换发生，从而将干预效果与随机重采样区分开。论文未披露模型规模、训练资源或推理硬件等具体信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "从45.3%提升至58.5%", "setting": "159个多轮BFCL V4任务，GPT-5.6 Sol，完整精确重放配对，95%任务自助法置信区间[8.2, 18.8]个百分点，未观察到成功转为失败的回归"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将执行边界修复重新定义为受约束的比较问题，而不是直接接受或拒绝提议动作。；提出负孪生（negative twin）：基于轨迹构造的反事实替代动作，并与原始提议进行成对验证。；当且仅当轨迹满足与局部失败假设相关的证据条件时，才考虑替换，并且要求孪生通过结构检查且成对验证器在两种候选顺序下都偏好孪生。；设计了精确重放配对评估方法，固定解析响应和动作直到第一个被接受的替换，从而将干预效应与重采样分离。

Abstract: A single locally plausible tool call can derail an otherwise successful agent trajectory. Suspicion alone does not justify intervention, because the replacement itself can introduce the very failure verification is meant to prevent. We introduce TwinCheck, an inference-time verification policy that considers replacement only when the trace satisfies an evidence condition tied to a trace-local failure hypothesis. It constructs a trace-grounded counterfactual alternative, a negative twin, and replaces the agent's proposal only if the twin passes structural checks and the pairwise verifier prefers it in both candidate orders. For paired evaluation, exact replay holds the agent's parsed responses and actions fixed until the first accepted replacement, separating intervention effects from resampling. In the primary analysis of 159 multi-turn BFCL V4 tasks with complete exact-replay pairs, the complete policy raises task success for GPT-5.6 Sol from 45.3% to 58.5% (95% task-bootstrap CI [8.2, 18.8]), with no observed success-to-failure regressions. Together, these findings recast execution-boundary repair as a constrained comparison, making the counterfactual action itself the object of verification.

</details>


### [85] [Harness as a Language: A Minimalist Agent Framework With Maximal Expressivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26891)
*Zhening Li,Joshua Liu,Mateja Vukelic,Nicole Shen,Supriya Lall,Amitayush Thakur,Alex Zhang,Omar Khattab,Jonathan Light,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAZ 是一个极简 LLM agent 框架，仅暴露单个原语 invoke 和 hooks。invoke 是满足两个条件的最简 loop：LLM 可写含递归 invoke 的任意代码，且所有可见内容均为代码环境变量。在长时程记忆任务（StuLife recall-heavy）上，纯 invoke 相比 Letta (MemGPT) 性能高 8% 且成本减半；在持续自我改进任务（AppWorld）上相比 ACE 高 4% 且成本更低。该工作展示了最小化 harness 在减少工程复杂性的同时保持表达力，对 agent 系统设计有启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 框架需要额外工程（如记忆系统、自我改进系统）来支持某些工作流；核心问题是：一个几乎等同于 agent loop 本身的最小化 harness 能在多大程度上完成这些原本需要专用系统才能完成的任务。

Method: JAZ 框架围绕单一 LLM 原语 invoke 构建。invoke 接受任意输入，由 LLM 在运行时生成并执行代码作为其实现。代码环境是可执行的，LLM 可以看到的所有内容——包括传给 invoke 的输入以及它与代码环境的交互历史——都是代码环境中的变量。该循环满足两个性质：(1) LLM 可以编写包含递归 invoke 的任意可执行代码；(2) LLM 可见的一切都是代码环境变量。框架提供一组内置 hooks，使程序员能够施加约束和监控，而无需手工设计工具或外部系统（如记忆或文件系统）。推理时，agent 反复调用 invoke，LLM 生成代码并执行，观察输出，然后继续；记忆和自我改进通过递归 invoke 和代码环境变量自然实现，不需要专用外部模块。论文从编程语言视角将 invoke 视为一个语言原语，其实现由 LLM 在每次调用时动态提供。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能（未指定具体指标）", "value": "比 Letta (MemGPT) 高 8%", "setting": "StuLife 的 recall-heavy 部分，成本为 Letta 的一半"}, {"metric": "性能（未指定具体指标）", "value": "比 ACE 高 4%", "setting": "AppWorld，成本更低"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出单原语 invoke：将 LLM 视为在每次调用时提供实现的函数，形成满足两个性质的最简 agent loop；将 LLM 可见的所有内容（输入、交互历史）作为代码环境中的变量，实现代码模态的广义化；内置 hooks 机制，允许程序员施加约束和监控，而无需手工设计工具或外部系统

Abstract: Modern language-model agents are built around the \textit{agent loop}, where the LLM is placed in an environment exposing a set of tools, and the LLM has full control over the workflow by alternating between tool calls and observing their output. However, certain workflows currently require additional engineering beyond the agent loop itself, such as memory systems and self-improving systems. We built an LLM agent framework, JAZ, to explore the extent to which a minimal harness that is little more than the agent loop itself can accomplish tasks these specialized systems are built for. JAZ exposes a single LLM-based primitive invoke and provides a set of built-in hooks that allow the programmer to apply constraints and monitoring. Generalizing existing code-mode agent loops, \texttt{invoke} is the simplest loop that satisfies two defining properties: (1) the LLM can write arbitrary executable code that can include recursive \texttt{invoke}; (2) everything visible to the LLM --- all inputs to \texttt{invoke} as well as its interaction history with the code environment --- are variables in the code environment. We motivate our design from first principles, viewing \texttt{invoke} as a language primitive representing a function whose implementation is provided at runtime by an LLM every time it is called. To validate the design of our core \texttt{invoke} primitive, we evaluate \texttt{invoke} --- with only prompting, no manually designed tools, harness, or external systems (e.g., memory or the file system) --- on workflows traditionally implemented through specialized external harnesses. On long-horizon workflows requiring recall beyond the context window, JAZ invoke outperforms Letta (MemGPT) by 8\% at half its cost on the recall-heavy portion of StuLife. On continual self-improvement, JAZ invoke outperforms ACE by 4\% at a lower cost on AppWorld.

</details>
